Explanatory Remarks:

1. Structural elements are enclosed in <>.

Text:

<1>
atha yajurvedīya kāṭhakasaṃhitā|

atha prathama sthānakam |

puroḍāśaḥ|
iṣe tvorje tvā vāyavasthopāyavasstha devo vassavitā prārpayatu śreṣṭhatamāya karmaṇe || āpyāyadhvamaghnyā devabhāgaṃ prajāvatīranamīvā ayakṣmāḥ || mā vasstena īśata māghaśaṃsaḥ pari vo rudrasya hetirvṛṇaktu || dhruvā asmin gopatau syāta bahvīryajamānasya paśūn pāhi yajamānasya paśupā asi || 1 ||

devasya tvā savituḥ prasave'śvinorbāhubhyāṃ pūṣṇo hastābhyāmādade goṣadasi pratyuṣṭaṃ rakṣaḥ pratyuṣṭārātiḥ preyamagāddhiṣaṇā barhiraccha manunā kṛtā svadhayā vitaṣṭā | urvantarikṣaṃ vīhīndrasya pariṣūtamāsi mādho mopari parusta ṛdhyāsamācchettā te mā riṣat || deva barhiśśatavalśaṃ viroha sahasravalśā vi vayaṃ ruhema | adityā rāsnāsīndrāṇyāssaṃnahanaṃ pūṣā te granthiṃ grathnātu sa te mā sthādindrasya tvā bāhubhyāmudyacche bṛhaspatestvā mūrdhnāharāmi devaṅgamamasi || tadāharanti kavayaḥ purastāddevebhyo juṣṭamiha barhirāsade || 2 ||

vasoḥ pavitramasi śatadhāraṃ vasūnāṃ pavitramasi sahasradhāram ||
ayakṣmā vaḥ prajayā sam̐sṛjāmi rāyaspoṣeṇa bahulā bhavantīḥ |
madhumad ghṛtavat pinvamānā jīvā jīvantīrupa vassadema ||
mātariśvano gharmo'si dyaurasi pṛthivyasi viśvadhāyāḥ pareṇa dhāmnāhrutāsi mā hvāssā viśvāyussā viśvavyacāssā viśvadhāyā hutasstoko huto drapso'gnaye bṛhate nākāya svāhā dyāvāpṛthivībhyām ||
saṃpṛcyadhvamṛtāvarīrūrmiṇā madhumattamāḥ | mandrā dhanasya sātayaḥ ||
indrasya tvā bhāgam̐ somenātanacmyadastamasi viṣṇave viṣṇo havyaṃ rakṣasvāpo jāgṛta || 3 ||

karmaṇe vāṃ vānaspatyamasi pratyuṣṭaṃ rakṣaḥ pratyuṣṭarātiḥ | urvantarikṣaṃ vīhi dhūrasi dhūrva dhūrvantaṃ yo'smān dhūrvati taṃ dhūrva yaṃ vayaṃ dhūrvāmastaṃ ca dhūrva || devānāmasi vahnitamaṃ sasnitamaṃ papritamaṃ juṣṭatamaṃ devahūtamaṃ viṣṇoḥ kramo'syahrutamasi havirdhānaṃ dṛm̐hasva mā hvārmiṃtrasya tvā cakṣuṣā prekṣa uru tvā vātāya devasya tvā savituḥ prasave'śvinorbāhubhyāṃ pūṣṇo hastābhyāmagnaye juṣṭaṃ nirvapāmi yachantu tvā pañca rakṣāyai tvā nārātyā idaṃ devānāmidaṃ nassaha svarabhivyakhyaṃ jyotirvaiśvānaraṃ dṛm̐hantāṃ duryāssvāhā pṛthivyāmurvantarikṣaṃ prehyagne havyaṃ rakṣasva || 4 ||

viṣṇormanasā pūte sthaḥ || devo vassavitotpunātvacchidreṇa pavitreṇa sūryasya raśmibhiḥ || devasya tvā savituḥ prasave'śvinorbāhubhyāṃ pūṣṇo hastābhyāmagnaye juṣṭaṃ prokṣāmi śundhantāṃ pātrāṇi devayajyāyai yadaśuddhaḥ parājaghāna tadva etena śundhantāmavadhūtaṃ rakṣo'vadhūtārātiradityāstvagasi prati tvā pṛthivī vettvadhiṣavaṇamasi vānaspatyaṃ prati tvādityāstvagvettu pṛthivīṃ dṛm̐hāgnestanūrāsa vāco visarjanaṃ devatābhyastvā devavītaye gṛhṇāmi bṛhad grāvāsi vānaspatyassa idaṃ devebhyo havyam̐ śamnīṣva suśami śamnīṣvādrirasi ślokakṛdapahataṃ rakṣo'pahatārātirvayam̐ saṃghātam̐ saṃghātam̐ saṃjayema varṣavṛddhamasi prati tvā varṣavṛddhaṃ vettu parāpūtaṃ rakṣaḥ parāpūtārātirnirasto aghaśam̐so vāyurva iṣa urje vivinaktu|| 5 ||

avadhūtaṃ rakṣo'vadhūtārātiradityāstvagasi prati tvā pṛthivī vettu dhiṣaṇāsi pārvatī prati tvādityāstvagvettu pṛthivīṃ dṛm̐ha dhiṣaṇāsi pārvateyī prati tvā pārvatī vettu divaṃ dṛm̐ha divasskambhadhānyamasi dhinuhi devān dhinuhi yajñaṃ dhinuhi yajñapatiṃ dhinuhi māṃ yajñanyaṃ prāṇāya vā vyānāya tvāpānāya tvā dīrghāmanu samṛtimāyuṣe tvā devo vassavitā hiraṇyapāṇiḥ pratigṛhṇātvadabdhena vaścakṣuṣāvapaśyāmi rāyaspoṣāya suprajāstvāya || 6 ||

nirdagdhaṃ rakṣo nirdagdhārātirapāgne agnimāmādaṃ jahi niṣkravyādam̐ sedhā devayajanaṃ vaha dhruvamasi pṛthivīṃ dṛm̐hāyurdehi prāṇaṃ dehi sajātānasmai yajamānāya paryūha dharuṇamasyantarikṣaṃ dṛm̐ha cakṣurdehi śrotraṃ dehi sajātānasmai yajamānāya paryūha dhartramasi divaṃ dṛm̐haujo dehi balaṃ dehi sajātānasmai yajamānāya paryūha dharmāsi diśo dṛm̐ha rayiṃ dehi poṣaṃ dehi sajātānasmai yajamānāya paryūha yantramasyāśā dṛm̐ha rūpaṃ dehi balaṃ dehi sajātānasmai yajamānāya paryūha citasstha paricito yajamānasya sajātā bhṛgūnāmāṅgirasāṃ tapasā tapyadhvam ||
yāni gharme kapālānyupacinvanti vedhasaḥ | pūṣṇastānyapi vrata indravāyū vimuñcatām||7||

devasya tvā savituḥ prasave'śvinorbāhubhyāṃ pūṣṇo hastābhyāmagnaye juṣṭam̐ saṃvapāmi samāpa oṣadhībhissamoṣadhayo rasena madhunā madhumatīḥ pṛcyantāṃ yadvo revatī revatyaṃ yadvo haviṣyā haviṣyaṃ yadvo jagatīrjagatyaṃ tenāsmai yajñapataya āśāsānā madhunā madhumatīssaṃpṛcyadhvaṃ janayatyai tvā makhasya śiro'si gharmo'si viśvāyururu prathasvoru te yajñapatiḥ prathatāṃ pratyuṣṭaṃ rakṣaḥ pratyuṣṭārātirdevastvā savitā śrapayatu varṣiṣṭhe'dhināke'gniste tanvaṃ mā hiṃsīdagne brahma gṛhīṣvaikatāya svāhā dvitīya svāhā tritāya svāhā || 8 ||

devasya tvā savituḥ prasave'śvinorbāhubhyāṃ pūṣṇo hastābhyāmādada indrasya bāhurasi dakṣiṇassahasrabhṛṣṭiśśatatejā vāyurasi tigmatejāḥ pṛthivi devayajanyoṣadhyāste mūlaṃ mā him̐siṣaṃ vrajaṃ gaccha gosthānaṃ varṣatu te dyaurbadhāna deva savitaḥ paramasyāṃ pṛthivyām̐ śatena pāśairyo'smān dipsati yaṃ vayaṃ dipsāmastamato mā maugdrapsaste dyāṃ mā skān vrajaṃ gaccha gosthānaṃ varṣatu te dyaurbadhāna deva savitaḥ paramasyāṃ pṛthivyām̐ śatena pāśairyo'smān dipsati yaṃ vayaṃ dipsāmastamato mā maugararurdyāṃ mā paptad vrajaṃ gaccha gosthānaṃ varṣatu te dyaurbadhāna deva savitaḥ paramasyāṃ pṛthivyām̐ śatena pāśairyo'smān dipsati yaṃ vayaṃ dipsāmastamato mā maugapārarumadevayajanaṃ pṛthivyā devayajanāñjahi || imāṃ naraḥ kṛṇuta vo devebhyo juṣṭāmadityā upasthe | imāṃ devā ajuṣanta viśve rāyaspoṣā yajamānaṃ viśantu| vasavastvā parigṛhṇantu gāyatreṇa chandasāṅgirasvadrudrāstvā parigṛhṇantu traiṣṭubhena cchandasāṅgirasvadādityāstvā parigṛhṇantu jāgatena cchandasāṅgirasvaddhā asi svadhā asi ||
purā krūrasya visṛpo virapśinnudādāya pṛthivīṃ jīradānum ||
tāmairayam̐ścandramasi svadhābhistāṃ dhīrāso anudṛśyāyajanta kavayaḥ || 9 ||

pṛśnyāḥ payo'si tasya te'kṣīyamānasya pinvamānasya pinvamānaṃ nirvapāmi ||
āśāsānā saumanasaṃ prajāṃ saubhāgyaṃ rayim | agneranuvratā bhūtvā saṃnahya sukṛtāya kam|| agne gṛhapata upa mā hvayasva devānāṃ patnīrupa mā hvayadhvamaditiriva tvā suputropaniṣadeyamindrāṇīvāvidhaveṣe tvādabdhena tvā cakṣuṣāvapaśyāmi rāyaspoṣāya suprajāstvāyorje tvāgnerjihvāsi supūrdevebhyo dhāmne dhāmne tvā yajuṣe yajuṣe havirasi vaiśvānaramunnītaśuṣmaiṃ satyaujāssaho nāmāsi sahasvārātim̐ sahasva pṛtanāyatassahasravīryamasi tanmārjitvājyasyājyamasi haviṣo havissatyābhighṛtamasi satyena tvābhighārayāmi || tejo'si śukramasi jyotirasi dhāmāsi priyaṃ devānāmanādhṛṣṭaṃ devayajanaṃ devatābhyastvā yajñiyebhyo gṛhṇāmi pratyuṣṭaṃ rakṣaḥ pratyārātiraniśitāsstha sapatnakṣayaṇīrāyuḥ prāṇaṃ mā nirdṛkṣaṃ vājinaṃ tvā vājinvājayatyāyai saṃmārjmi cakṣuḥ śrotraṃ mā nirdṛkṣaṃ vājinīṃ tvā vājini vājayatyāyai saṃmārjmi prajāṃ yoniṃ mā nirdṛkṣaṃ vājinīṃ tvā vājayatyāyai saṃmārjmi goṣṭhaṃ yajamānasya rāyaspoṣaṃ mā nirdṛkṣaṃ vājinīṃ tvā vājini vājayatyāyai saṃmārjmi || 10 ||

devīrāpo agreguvaḥ premaṃ yajñaṃ nayata pra yajñapatiṃ tirata yuṣmānindro'vṛṇīta vṛtratūrye yūyamindramavṛṇīdhvaṃ vṛtratūrye mekṣitāsstha kṛṣṇo'syākhareṣṭhā agnaye tvā juṣṭaṃ prokṣāmi vedisi barhiṣe tvā juṣṭaṃ prokṣāmi barhirasi srugbhyastvā juṣṭaṃ prokṣāmi pitṝṇāṃ bhāgadheyīssthorjā pṛthivīṃ gacchata viṣṇosstupo'syūrṇamradaḥ prathasva svāsasthaṃ devebhyo gandhavoṃ'si viśvāvasurviśvasmādīṣato yajamānasya paridhiriḍa īḍita indrasya bāhurasi dakṣiṇo yajamānasya paridhiriḍa īḍito mitrāvaruṇau tvā paridhattāṃ dhruveṇa dharmaṇeḍa īḍitam ||
vītihotraṃ tvā kave dyumantassamidhīmahi | agne bṛhantamadhvare ||
sūryastvā purastāt pātu kasyāścidabhiśastyāḥ | varṣiṣṭhe'dhi nāke ||
saviturbāhū stho devajanānāṃ vidharaṇirvasūnāṃ rudrāṇāmādityānāṃ sadanamasi juhvehi ghṛtācī dyaurjanmanāditiracchinnapatrā priyā devānāṃ priyeṇa dhāmnā priye sadasi sīdopabhṛdehi ghṛtācyantarikṣaṃ janmanāditiracchinnapatrā priyā devānāṃ priyeṇa dhāmnā priye sadasi sīda dhruva ehi ghṛtācī pṛthivī janmanādiraticchinnapatrā priyā devānāṃ priyeṇa dhāmnā priye sadasi sīdarṣabho'si śākvaro vāyurjanmanāditiracchinnapatraḥ priyo devānāṃ priyeṇa dhāmnā priye sadasi sīda dhuvā asadannṛtasya yonau sukṛtasya loke tā viṣṇo pāhi pāhi yajñaṃ pāhi yajñapatiṃ pāhi māṃ yajñanyam || 11 ||

bhuvanamasi viprathasvāgniryaṣṭedaṃ namo juhvehyagnistvāhvayati devayajyāya upabhṛdehi devastvā savitāhvayati devayajyāyā aṅghriṇā viṣṇū mā vāmavakramiṣaṃ pāhi māgne duścaritādā mā sucarite bhaja vijihāthāṃ mā mā saṃtāptaṃ lokaṃ me lokakṛtau kṛṇutaṃ viṣṇossthāmna ita indro vīryamakṛṇot|| ūrdhvo adhvaro divispṛgahruto yajño yajñapateḥ || indravān bṛhadbhāssvāhā || saṃ jyotiṣā jyotirvājasyāhaṃ prasavenāgnīṣomābhyāṃ devatayojjayāmi vājasyāhaṃ prasavenāgnīṣomābhyāṃ devatayāsuṃ pratinude ||
vājasya mā prasavenodgrābhenodajīgrabham | athā sapatnām̐ indro me nigrābhenādharām̐ akaḥ||
udgrābhaśca nigrābhaśca brahma devām̐ avīvṛdhat |
athā sapatnānindrāgnī me viṣūcīnān vyasyatām ||
pṛthivyai tvāntarikṣāya tvā dive tvārthaṃ rihāṇā vyantu vayaḥ ||
marutāṃ pṛṣatī vaśā pṛśnirbhūtvā divaṃ gaccha| tato no vṛṣṭimeraya ||
ahīnaḥ prāṇaścakṣuṣpā asi cakṣurme pāhi ||
yaṃ paridhiṃ paryadhatthā agne deva paṇibhiridhyamānaḥ |
taṃ ta etamanu joṣaṃ bharāmyeṣa nettvapacetayātai ||
agneḥ pātha upehīndrasya pātha upehi viśveṣāṃ devānāṃ pātha upehi yajamānaṃ prathata ||
sam̐srāvabhāgāsstheṣā bṛhantaḥ prastareṣṭhāḥ paridheyāśca devāḥ |
yajñasya gopā uta rakṣitārassvāhā devā amṛtā mādayantām ||
agne'dabdhāyo'śīrtatano pāhi vidyotpāhi prasityāḥ pāhi duriṣṭyāḥ pāhi duradmanyā aviṣaṃ naḥ pituṃ kṛdhi sudhīnyonīn suṣadāṃ pṛthivīm̐ svāhā devā gātuvido gātuṃ vittvā gātumita manasaspata imaṃ devayajñam̐ svāhā vāci svāhā vāte dhāḥ || 12 ||


|| iti śrīyajuṣi kāṭhake carakaśākhāyāmiṭhimikāyāṃ puroḍāśo nāma prathamaṃ sthānakaṃ saṃpūrṇam ||1||
<2>
atha dvitīyaṃ sthānakam ||

adhvaram |
ārdradānavasstha jīvadānavassthondatīriha māvatāpa undantu jīvase dīrghāyutvāya vacase oṣadhe trāyasvainam̐ svadhite mainam̐ hiṃsīrdevāyurimān pravape svastyuttarām̐ aśīya ||
śaṃ na āpo dhanvanyāśśaṃ nassantvanūpyāḥ | śaṃ nassamudriyā āpaśśamu nassantu kūpyāḥ|| āpo asmān mātarassūdayantu ghṛtena mā ghṛtapvaḥ punantu |
viśvam̐ ha ripraṃ pravahanti devīrudidābhyaḥ śucirā pūta emi ||
somasya dātramasi tanūrasi tanvaṃ me pāhi mahīnāṃ payo'syapāmoṣadhīnāṃ raso varcodhā asi varco me dhehi mitrasyāsi kanīnikāntarahaṃ tvayā dveṣamantararātīrdadhe cakṣurdhā asi cakṣurme dhehi citpatistvā punātu vākpatistvā punātu devastvā savitā punātvacchidreṇa pavitreṇa sūryasya raśmibhistasya te pavitrapate pavitreṇa yasmai kaṃ pune tacchakeyam || 1 ||

ākūtyai prayuje agnaye svāhā medhāyai manase agnaye svāhā dīkṣāyai tapase agnaye svāhā sarasvatyai pūṣṇe agnaye svāhāpo devīrbṛhatīrviśvaśambhuvo dyāvāpṛthivī uro antarikṣa bṛhaspataye haviṣā vidhema svāhā ||
viśvo devasya neturmarto vareta sakhyam | viśvo rāya iṣudhyati dyumne vareta puṣyatu || svāhendrāgnī dyāvāpṛthivī āpa oṣadhaya uro antarikṣānu me dīkṣā dīkṣāpataya manyadhvamanu tapastapaspatayo'gniśca deva sūryeṣamūrj dadhātana deva savitastvaṃ dīkṣāṇāṃ dīkṣāpatirasītthaṃ mā santaṃ pāhyā modṛcaḥ pāhi || 2 ||

ṛksāmayośśilpe sthaste vāmārabha ā modṛcaḥ pātam̐ śarmāsi śarma meṃ yaccha namaste astu mā mā him̐sīḥ ||
imāṃ dhiyam̐ śikṣamānasya deva kratuṃ dakṣaṃ varuṇam̐ sam̐śiśādhi ||
yayāti viśvā duritā tarema sutarmāṇamadhi nāvaṃ ruheyam ||
imām̐ su nāvamāruhamariṣṭāṃ pārayiṣṇum | śatāritrām̐ svastaye ||
sutrāmāṇaṃ pṛthivīṃ dyāmanehasam̐ suśarmāṇamaditiṃ supraṇītim ||
daivīṃ nāvam̐ svaritrāmanāgasamasravantīmāruhema svastaye ||
ūrgasyāṅgirasyūrṇamradā ūrjaṃ mayi dhehi mā mā him̐sīssomasya nīvirasi mā mā him̐sīrindrasya yonirasi mā mā him̐sīḥ kṛṣyai tvā sumanasyāyai kṛṣiṃ susasyāmutkṛdhi viṣṇośśarmāsi śarma yajamānasya śarma me yaccha namaste astu mā mā hiṃsīrnakṣatrāṇāṃ mātīkāśāt pāhi sūpasthāvā vanaspata ūrdhvo mā pāhyodṛcassvāhā yajñamanasassvāhā divassvāhā pṛthivyāssvāhororantarikṣāt svāhā vātāt pratigṛhṇāmi yajñam || 3 ||

vrataṃ kṛṇuta tacchakeyam ||
daivīṃ dhiyaṃ manāmahe sumṛḍīkāmabhiṣṭaye | varcodhāṃ viśvadhāyasam̐ sutīrthā no asadvaśe || ye devā manujātā manuyujassudakṣā dakṣapitaraḥ | te naḥ pāntu te no'vantu tebhyassvāhā ||
agne tvam̐ su jāgṛhi vayam̐ su mandiṣāmahe | gopāya no aprayucchan prabudhe naḥ puraskṛdhi||
tvamagne vratapā asi deva ā martyeṣvā | tvaṃ yajñeṣvīḍyaḥ ||
pūṣā sanīnām̐ somo rādhasāṃ rāsveyatsomā bhūyo bhara devassavitā vasorvasudāvā mā pṛṇan pūrtyā virādhiṣṭa māhamāyuṣā varcasā virādhiṣi || devīrāpo apāṃ napādya ūrmirhaviṣya indriyāvām̐staṃ vo mā kramiṣam̐ sakṣemahi yā te yajñiyā tanūstayāroha ||
ayaṃ te yonirṛtviyo yato jāto arocathāḥ ||
taṃ jānanagna āroha tato no vardhayā rayim ||
edamaganma devayajanaṃ pṛthivyā yatra devā ajuṣanta viśve ||
ṛksāmābhyām̐ saṃtaranto yajurbhī rāyaspoṣe samiṣā madema || 4 ||

iyaṃ te śukra tanūridaṃ varcastayā saṃbhava bhrājaṃ gaccha | jūrasi dhṛtā manasā juṣṭā viṣṇave tasyāste satyasavasaḥ prasave tanvo yantramaśīya svāhā || śukramasi candramasyamṛtamasi vaiśvadevam̐ havirasi cidasi manā asi dhīrasi dakṣiṇāsi yajñiyāsi kṣatriyāsyāditirasyubhayataśśīrṣṇī sā mā suprācī supratīcī bhava mitrastvā padi badhnātu pūṣādhvanaspātvindrāyādhyakṣāyānu tvā mātā manyatāmanu pitānu bhrātā sagarbhyo'nu sakhā sayūthyaḥ || sā devi devamacchehīndrāya somaṃ rudrastvāvartayatu svasti somasakhā punarehi vasvyasi rudrāsyaditirasyādityāsi candrāsi rudrāsi bṛhaspatistvā sumne ramṇātu rudro vasubhirācake pṛthivyāstvā mūrdhannājigharmiṃ devayajana iḍāyāspade ghṛtavati svāhedamahaṃ rakṣaso grīvā apikṛntāmīdamahaṃ yo nassamāno yo'samāno'rātīyati tasya grīvā apikṛntāmyasme ramasvāsme te rāyo me rāyastava tava rāyo māhaṃ rāyaspoṣeṇa viyoṣaṃ saṃ devī devyorvaśyākhyata suretā reto dadhānāstvaṣṭṛmantastvā sapema mā ma āyuḥ pramoṣīrmo ahaṃ tava vīraṃ videya tava saṃdṛśi ||5||

deva sūrya somaṃ kreṣyāmastaṃ te prabūmastaṃ tvaṃ viśvebhyo devebhya ṛtūn kalpaya dakṣiṇāḥ kalpaya yatharṣi yathādevatamāsmāko'si śukraste grahaḥ ||
abhi tyaṃ deva savitāramoṇyoḥ kavikratum | arcāmi satyasavaṃ ratnadhāmabhi priyaṃ matim ||
ūrdhvā yasyāmatirbhā adidyutatsavīmani | hiraṇyapāṇiramimīta sukratuḥ kṛpā svaḥ || prajābhyastvā prajāsastvānuprāṇantu prajāstvamanu prāṇihi somaṃ te krīṇāmyūrjasvantaṃ payasvantaṃ vīryāvantaṃ barhvagham̐ śobhamānam || śukraṃ te śukreṇa krīṇāmi candraṃ candreṇāmṛtamamṛtena śakma yatte gaurasme te candrāṇi tapasastanūrasi prajāpatevarṇassahasrapoṣaṃ puṣyantī parameṇa paśunā krīyase'sme te bandhurmitro na ehi sumitradhā indrasyorumāviśa dakṣiṇamuśannuśantam̐ syonassyonam || svānnabhrāḍaṅghāre bambhāre hasta suhasta kṛśāna ete vassomakrayaṇāstān rakṣadhvaṃ mā vo dabhan |
udāyuṣā svāyuṣodrasenauṣadhīnām | utparjanyasya vṛṣṭyodasthāmamṛtām̐ anu || urvantarikṣaṃ vīhi ||
api panthāmaganmahi svastigāmanehasam | yena viśvāḥ pari dviṣo vṛṇakti vindate vasu ||
adityāstvagasyadityāssadane sīda ||
astabhnād dyāmṛṣabho viśvavedā amimīta varimāṇaṃ pṛthivyāḥ |
āsīdadviśvā bhuvanāni samrāḍ viśvettāni varuṇasya vratāni ||
vaneṣu vyantarikṣaṃ tatāna vājamarvatsu payo aghnyāsu |
hṛtsu kratuṃ varuṇo vikṣvagniṃ divi sūryamadadhāt somamadrau ||
sūryasya cakṣurāruhamagnerakṣṇaḥ kanīnikām |
yadetaśebhirīyase bhrājamāno vipaścitā||6||

usrā etaṃ dhūrbādhā avīrahaṇā anaśśū brahmacaudanau yajñasyāyuḥ pratirantau svasti gṛhān gacchataṃ dhūrasi dhūrva dhūrvantaṃ yo'smān dhūrvati taṃ dhūrva yaṃ vayaṃ dhūrvāmastaṃ ca dhūrva vāruṇamasi varuṇasya skambhanyasi pracyavasva bhuvanaspate viśvānyabhi dhāmāni mā tvā paripariṇo vidanmā paripanthino mā tvā vṛkā aghāyavo vidan | śyeno bhūtvā parāpata yajamānasya no gṛhe sam̐skṛtam ||
namo mitrasya varuṇasya cakṣase maho devāya taṃ tadṛtam̐ saparyata ||
dūredṛśe devajātāya ketave divasputrāya sūryāya śam̐sata ||
vāruṇamasi varuṇastvottabhnātu varuṇasya skambhasarjanyasi pramukto varuṇasya pāśo varuṇo'si dhṛtavrato'cchinnapatraḥ prajā upāvaroha varuṇasyartasadanamasyadityāstvagasyadityāssadane sīda ||
niṣasāda dhṛtavrato varuṇaḥ pastyāsvā | sāmrājyāya sukratuḥ ||
vāruṇamasi varuṇāya tvā || 7 ||

agnestanūrasi viṣṇave tvā somasya tanūrasi viṣṇave tvātitherātithyamasi viṣṇave tvāgnaye tvā rāyaspoṣade viṣṇave tvā śyenāya tvā somabhṛte viṣṇave tvāpataye tvā paripataye gṛhṇāmi tanūnaptre śakmane śākvarāya śakmanojiṣṭhāyānādhṛṣṭamasyanādhṛṣyaṃ devānāmojo'bhiśastipā anabhiśastenyamañjasā satyamupāgām̐ suvite mā dhā agne vratapā asme vratapāstve vratapāḥ punarvratapā vratināṃ vratāni yāgne mama tanūreṣā sā tvayi yā tava tanūriyam̐ sā mayi saha nau vratapā vratināṃ vratāni || yā te agne rudriyā tanūstayā naḥ pāhi tasyai te svāhām̐śuram̐śuste deva somāpyāyatāmindrāyaikadhanaa | tubhyamindraḥ pyāyatāmā tvamindrāya pyāyasvāpyāyaya sakhīn sanyā medhayā svasti te deva soma sutyāmaśīya svastyodṛcameṣṭā rāya eṣṭā vāmāni preṣe bhagāyartamṛtavādibhyo namo dive namaḥ pṛthivyai yā te agne'yāśayā tanūrvarṣiṣṭhā gahaneṣṭhā varṣiṣṭhā gahvareṣṭhā ugraṃ vaco'pāvadhīt tveṣaṃ vaco'pāvadhīt svāhā yā te agne rajāśayā harāśayā tanūvarṣiṣṭhā gahaneṣṭhā varṣiṣṭhā gahvareṣṭhā ugraṃ vaco'pāvadhīt tveṣaṃ vaco'pāvadhīt svāhā || 8 ||

devasya tvā savituḥ prasave'śvinorbāhubhyāṃ pūṣṇo hastābhyāmādade nāryasīdamahaṃ rakṣaso grīvā apikṛntāmīdamahaṃ yo nassamāno yo'samāno'rātīyati tasya grīvā apikṛntāmi taptāyanyasi vittāyanyasyavatānmā nāthitamavatād vyathitamagne aṅgiro yo'syāṃ pṛthivyāmasyāyunā nāmnehi yatte'nādhṛṣṭaṃ nāmānādhṛṣyaṃ tena tvādadhe'gne aṅgiro yo dvitīyasyāṃ yastṛtīyasyāṃ pṛthivyāmasyāyunā nāmnehi yatte'nādhṛṣṭaṃ nāmānādhṛṣyaṃ tena tvādadhe videdagnirnabho nāma siṃhyasi mahiṣyasi devebhyaḥ prathasva devebhyaḥ kalpasva devebhyaḥ śundhasva devebhyaḥ śumbhasva ||
vibhrāḍ bṛhat pibatu somyaṃ madhvāyurdadhadyajñapatā avihrutam ||
vātajūto yo abhirakṣati tmanā prajāḥ piparti bahudhā virājati ||
indraghoṣāstvā vasavaḥ purastāt pāntu manojavāstvā pitaro dakṣiṇataḥ pāntu pracetāstvā rudraiḥ paścāt pātu viśvakarmā tvādityairuttarāt pātu tvaṣṭā tvā rūpairupariṣṭāt pātu siṃhyasi sapatnasāhī svāhā siṃhyasi rāyaspoṣavanissvāhā sim̐hyasi suprajāvanissvāhā siṃhyasyādityavanissvāhā siṃhyasyāvaha devān devāyate yajamānāya svāhā bhūtebhyastvā viśvāyurasi pṛthivīṃ skabhānācyutakṣidasyantarikṣam̐ skabhāna dhuvakṣidasi divam̐ skabhānāgneḥ kulāyamasyagneḥ purīṣamasi yajñaḥ pratyaṣṭhāt ||
manuṣvattvā nidhīmahi manuṣvatsamidhīmahi | agne manuṣvadaṅgiro devān devayate yaja||9||

yuñjate mana uta yuñjate dhiyo viprā viprasya bṛhato vipaścitaḥ |
vi hotrā dadhe vayunāvideka inmahī devasya savituḥ pariṣṭutiḥ ||
devaśrutau devaṣvāghoṣethām || idaṃ viṣṇurvicakrame tredhā nidadhe padam | samūḍhamasya pām̐sure ||
iḍāvatī dhenumatī hi bhūtaṃ sūyavasinī mānuṣe yaśasye |
vyaṣṭabhnā rodasī viṣṇa ete dādhartha pṛthivīmabhito mayūkhaiḥ ||
vaiṣṇavamasi viṣṇustvottabhnātu ||
divo vā viṣṇa uta vā pṛthivyā maho vā viṣṇa urorantarikṣāt ||
hastau pṛṇasva bahubhirvasavyairāprayaccha dakṣiṇādota savyāt ||
viṣṇornu kaṃ vīryāṇi pravocaṃ yaḥ pārthivāni vimame rajam̐si ||
yo askabhāyaduttaraṃ sadhasthaṃ vicakramāṇastredhorugāyaḥ ||
viṣṇoḥ pṛṣṭhamasi viṣṇo rarāṭamasi viṣṇośśnaptre stho viṣṇossyūrasi viṣṇordhruvo'si vaiṣṇavamasi viṣṇave tvā ||
pra tadviṣṇusstavate vīryeṇa mṛgo na bhīmaḥ kucaro giriṣṭhāḥ |
yasyoruṣu triṣu vikramaṇeṣvadhikṣiyanti bhuvanāni viśvā || 10

devasya tvā savituḥ prasave'śvinorbāhubhyāṃ pūṣṇo hastābhyāmādada idamahaṃ rakṣaso grīvā aṃpikṛntāmīdamahaṃ yo nassamāno yo'samāno'rātīyati tasya grīvā apikṛntāmi bṛhannasi bṛhadgrāvā bṛhatīmindrāya vācaṃ vada rakṣohaṇaṃ valagahanaṃ vaiṣṇavīmidamahaṃ tān valagānudvapāmi yāssamāno yānasamāno nicakhānedamahaṃ tān valagānudvapāmi yānnassabandhuryānasabandhurnicakhānedamahaṃ tān valagānudvapāmi yānnassanābhiryānasanābhirnicakhānedamahaṃ tān valagānudvapāmi yānanassvo yānaraṇo nicakhāna rāḍasi virāḍasi samrāḍasi svarāḍasi gāyatreṇa cchandasāvabāḍho yaṃ dviṣmastraiṣṭubhena jāgatenānuṣṭubhena pāṅktena cchandasāvabāḍho yaṃ dviṣmaḥ || kimatra bhadraṃ tannau saha nirasto valagovavāḍho durasya rakṣoghno valagaghnaḥ prokṣāmi vaiṣṇavān rakṣoghno balagaghno'vasiñcāmi vaiṣṇavān rakṣoghno balagaghno'vastṛṇāmi vaiṣṇavān rakṣohaṇau valagahanau prokṣāmi vaiṣṇavīṃ rakṣohaṇau valagahanā upadadhāmi vaiṣṇavī rakṣohaṇau valagahanau paryūhāmi vaiṣṇavī rakṣohā tvā valagahā prokṣāmi vaiṣṇavaṃ rakṣohā tvā valagahā stṛṇāmi vaiṣṇavaṃ rakṣoghno valagaghnassam̐sādayāmi vaiṣṇavān || 11 ||

devasya tvā savituḥ prasave'śvinorbāhubhyāṃ pūṣṇo hastābhyāmādada idamahaṃ rakṣaso grīvā api kṛntāmīdamahaṃ yo nassamāno yo'samāno'rātīyati tasya grīvā apikṛntāmi dive tvāntarikṣāya tvā pṛthivyai tvā śundhantāṃ lokāḥ pitṛṣadanā yavo'si yāvayāsmad dveṣaṃ yāvayārātiṃ pitṛṣadanaṃ tvā lokamāstṛṇāmyud dyām̐ stabhānāntarikṣaṃ pṛṇa dṛm̐hasva pṛthivyāṃ nitānastvā māruto nihantu mitrāvaruṇayordhruveṇa dharmaṇā || brahmavaniṃ tvā kṣatravaniṃ devavani sajātavaniṃ rāyaspoṣavaniṃ paryūhāmi brahma dṛm̐ha kṣatraṃ dṛm̐ha prajāṃ dṛm̐ha rāyaspoṣaṃ dṛm̐ha ghṛtena dyāvāpṛthivī āpṛṇethāṃ viśvajanasya cchāyāsīndrasya syūrasīndrasya dhuvo'syaindramasīndrāya tvā ||
pari tvā girvaṇo gira imā bhavantu viśvataḥ |
vṛddhāyumanu vṛddhayo juṣṭā bhavantu juṣṭaya: || 12 ||

vibhūrasi pravāhaṇo vahnirasi havyavāhanaśśvātro'si pracetāstutho'si viśvavedā uśigasi kaviraṅghārirasi bambhāriravasyurasi duvasvāñchundhyurasi mārjālīyassamrāḍasi kṛśānuḥ pariṣadyo'syāstavyo nabho'si pratakvāsaṃmaṣṭo'si havyasūdana ṛtadhāmāsi svarjyotissamudro'si viśvavyacā ajosyaikapādahirasi budhnyaḥ kavyo'si kavyavāhano raudreṇānīkena pāhi māgne pipṛhi māgne namaste astu mā mā hiṃsīḥ || 13 ||

agnirvṛtrāṇi jaṅghanad draviṇasyurvipanyayā | samiddhaśśukra āhutaḥ ||
tvam̐ somāsi satpatistvaṃ rājota vṛtrahā | tvaṃ bhadra asi kratuḥ ||
agne yaṃ yajñamadhvaraṃ viśvataḥ paribhūrasi | sa iddeveṣu gacchati ||
tvaṃ nassoma viśvato rakṣā rājananghāyataḥ | na riṣyettvāvatassakhā ||
sa tvaṃ viprāya dāśuṣe rayiṃ dehi sahasriṇam | agne vīravatīmiṣam ||
vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi vṛṣā deva vṛṣavrataḥ | vṛṣā dharmāṇi dadhiṣe |||
ā no agne sucetunā rayiṃ viśvāyupoṣasam | mārḍīkaṃ dhehi jīvase |||
gayasphāno amīvahā vasuvit puṣṭivardhanaḥ | sumitrassoma no bhava ||
tvamagne saprathā asi juṣṭo hotā vareṇyaḥ | tvayā yajñaṃ vitanvate ||
tvam̐ soma mahe bhagaṃ tvaṃ yūna ṛtāyate | dakṣaṃ dadāsi jīvase ||
agne rakṣā ṇo am̐hasaḥ prati ṣma deva rīṣataḥ | tapiṣṭairajaro daha ||
agne nakṣatramajaramā sūryaṃ rohayo divi | dadhajjyotirjanebhyaḥ ||
ādit pratnasya retaso jyotiṣpaśyanti vāsaram | paro yadidhyate divā ||
agniḥ pratnena manmanā stambhānastanvam̐ svām | kavirvipreṇa vāvṛdhe ||
soma yāste mayobhuva ūtayassanti dāśuṣe | tābhirno'vitā bhava ||
apsvagne ||
apsu me somo abravīdantarviśvāni bheṣajā| agniṃ ca viśvaśambhuvam ||
agnī rakṣaॉm̐si sedhati śukraśociramartyaḥ | śuciṣpāvaka īḍyaḥ ||
ehyū ṣu bravāṇi te'gna itthetarā giraḥ | ebhirvardhāsa indubhiḥ ||
imaṃ yajñamidaṃ vaco jujuṣāṇa upāgahi | soma tvaṃ no vṛdhe bhava ||
udagne śucayastava ||
nyagniṃ jātavedasam̐ hotravāhaṃ yaviṣṭhyam | dadhātā devamatvijam ||
agnimīḍe purohitaṃ yajñasya devamṛtvijam | hotāraṃ ratnadhātamam ||
soma gīrbhiṣṭvā vayaṃ vardhayāmo vacovidaḥ | sumṛḍīko naḥ āviśa || 14 ||

juṣṭo damūnā atithirduroṇa imaṃ no yajñamupayāhi vidvān |
viśvā agne abhiyujo vihatyā śatrūyatāmābharā bhojanāni ||
agne śardha mahate saubhagāya tava dyumnānyuttamāni santu |
saṃ jāspatyai suyamamākṛṇuṣva śatrūyatāmabhitiṣṭhā mahām̐si ||
sam̐ samidyuvase vṛṣannagne viśvānyarya ā | īḍaspade samidhyase sa no vasūnyābhara ||
sakhāyassaṃ vassamyañcamiṣaṃ stomaṃ cāgnaye | vārṣiṣṭhāya kṣitīnāmūrjo naptre sahasvate ||
agnistuviśravastamaṃ tuvibrahmāṇamuttamam | atūrtam̐ śrāvayat patiṃ putraṃ dadāti dāśuṣe ||
agnirdadāti satpatiṃ sāsāha yo yudhā nṛbhiḥ |
agniratyaṃ raghuṣyadaṃ jetāramaparājitam ||
preddho agna imo agne |
uta no brahmannaviṣa uktheṣu devahūtamaḥ | śaṃ naḥ śocā marudvṛdho'gne sahasrasātamaḥ ||
nū no rāsva sahasravat tokavat puṣṭimadvasu | dyumadagne suvīryaṃ varṣiṣṭhamanupakṣitam ||
tvāṃ citraśravastamaṃ tvāmagne haviṣmanto vi pājasā ||
bhavā no agne'vitota gopā bhavā vayaskṛduta no vayodhāḥ |
rāsvā ca nassumaho havyadātiṃ trāsvota nastanvo aprayucchan ||
havyavāhamabhimātiṣāhaṃ rakṣohaṇaṃ pṛtanāsu jiṣṇum |
jyotiṣmantaṃ dīdyataṃ puraṃdhimagniṃ sviṣṭakṛtamāhuvema ||
sviṣṭamagne abhi tadgṛṇīhi viśvāśca deva pṛtanā abhiṣyāḥ |
sugaṃ nu panthāṃ pradiśan vibhāhi jyotiṣmaddhehyajaraṃ na āyuḥ ||
somasya mā tavasaṃ vakṣyagne vahniṃ cakartha vidathe yajadhyai |
devī acchā dīdyadyuñje adriṃ śamāye agne tanvaṃ juṣasva ||
śrīṇāmudāraḥ ||
yacciddhi te puruṣatrā yaviṣṭhācittibhiścakṛmā kaccidāgaḥ |
kṛdhī ṣvasmā aditeranāgānvyenam̐si śiśratho viśvagaganne ||
mahaścidagna enaso abhīka ūrvāddevānāmuta martyānām |
mā te sakhāyassadamidriṣāma yacchā tokāya tanayāya śaṃ yoḥ ||
yaṃ tvā devāpiḥ śuśucāno agna ārṣṭiṣeno manuṣyassamīdhe |
viśvebhirdevairanumadyamānaḥ pra parjanyamīrayā vṛṣṭimantam ||
agne bādhasva vi mṛdho vi durgahāpāmīvāmapa rakṣām̐si sedha |
asmāt samudrādbṛhato divo no'pāṃ bhūmānamupa nassṛjeha ||
yathā ha tyadvasavo gauryaṃ citpadi pitāmamuñcatā yajatrāḥ |
evo ṣvasmanmuñcatā vyam̐haḥ pra tāryagne prataraṃ na āyuḥ ||
trīṇyāyūm̐ṣi tava jātavedastisra ājānīruṣasaste agne |
tābhirdevānāmavo yakṣi vidvānathā bhava yajamānāya śaṃ yoḥ ||
piprīhi devām̐ uśato yaviṣṭha vidvām̐ ṛtūnṛtupate yajeha |
ye daivyā ṛtvijastebhiragne tvaṃ hotṝṇāmasyāyajiṣṭhaḥ ||
ā devānāmapi panthāmaganma yacchaknavāma tadanu pravoḍhum |
agnirvidvān saṃ yajātsedu hotā so adhvarān sa ṛtūn kalpayāti ||
vi jyotiṣā bṛhatā bhātyagnirāvirviśvāni kṛṇute mahitvā ||
prādevīrmāyāssahate durevāḥ śiśīte śṛṅge rakṣase vinakṣe ||
sādhvīmakardevahūtiṃ no adya yajñasya jihvāmavidāma guhyām |
sa āyurāgāt surabhirvasāno mahīmakardyumnahūtiṃ no adya ||
agnirhotā nyasīdadyajīyānupasthe mātussurabhā u loke |
yuvā kaviḥ puruniṣṭhā ṛtāvā dhartā kṛṣṭīnāmut madhya iddhaḥ ||
agniṃ dūtaṃ purodadhe havyavāhamupabruve | devām̐ āsādayādiha ||
agnirhotā purohito'dhvarasya vicarṣaṇiḥ | sa veda yajñamānuṣak || 15 | |


iti śrīyajuṣi kāṭhake carakaśākhāyāmiṭhimikāyāmadhvaraṃ nāma dvitīyaṃ sthānakaṃ saṃpūrṇam || 2 ||
<3>
atha tṛtīyaṃ sthānakam |

jyotirikam ||
jyotirāsi viśvarūpaṃ marutāṃ pṛṣatī devebhyastvā yajñiyebhyo gṛhṇāmi ||
tvam̐ soma tanūkṛdbhyo dveṣebhyo'nyakṛtebhyaḥ | uru yantāsi varūtham ||
svāhā juṣāṇo apturājyasya vetu svāhā ||
agne naya supathā rāye asmān viśvāni deva vayunāni vidvān | yuyodhyasmajjuhurāṇameno bhūyiṣṭhāṃ te namauktiṃ vidhema ||
uru viṣṇo vikramasvoru kṣayāya naskṛdhi | ghṛtaṃ ghṛtayone piba pra pra yajñapatiṃ tira || svāhorvantarikṣaṃ vīhyadityāstvagasyadityāssadane sīda || deva savitareṣa te somastaṃ rakṣasva mā tvā dabhannetattvaṃ deva soma devānupāgā idamahaṃ manuṣyān saha prajayā saha rāyaspoṣeṇa namo devebhyassvadhā pitṛbhyo nirdruho nirvaruṇasya pāśānmukṣīya svarabhivyakhyaṃ jyotirvaiśvānaramagne vratapā asme vratapāstve vratapā vratināṃ vratāni yāgne mama tanūstvayyabhūdiyam̐ sā mayi yā tava tanūrmayyabhūdeṣā sā tvayi yathāyathaṃ no vratapā vratināṃ vratāni svāhā yajñaṃ manasi svāhā divi svāhā pṛthivyām̐ svāhorā antarikṣe svāhā vāte vāci visṛje || 1 ||

uru viṣṇo vikramasvoru kṣayāya naskṛdhi | ghṛtaṃ ghṛtayone piba pra pra yajñapatiṃ tira ||
svāhātyanyānagāṃ nānyānupāgāṃ yānnājujoṣaṃ pari tānavṛjamarvāktvā parebhyo'vidaṃ paro'varebhyastaṃ tvā juṣāmahe vanaspate devayajyāyai juṣṭaṃ viṣṇave viṣṇave tvā devastvā savitā madhvānaktvoṣadhe trāyasvainam̐ svadhite maiṃnam̐ him̐sīrdyāṃ mā lekhīrantarikṣaṃ mā hiṃsīḥ pṛthivyā saṃbhava ||
vanaspate śatavalśo biroha sahasravalśā vi vayaṃ ruhema ||
yaṃ tvāyam̐ svadhatistetajānaḥ praṇināya mahate saubhagāya |
acchinnarāyam̐ suvīraḥ || 2 ||

devasya tvā savituḥ prasave'śvinorbāhubhyāṃ pūṣṇo hastābhyāmadada idamahaṃ rakṣaso grīvā apikṛntāmīdamahaṃ yo nassamāno yo'samāno'rātīyati tasya grīvā apikṛntāmi pṛthivyai tvāntarikṣāya tvā dive tvā śundhantāṃ lokāḥ pitṛṣadanā yavo'si yāvayāsmaddveṣaṃ yāvayārātiṃ pitṛṣadanaṃ tvā lokamāstṛṇāmi svāveśo'syagregā netṝṇāṃ vanaspatistvādhiṣṭhāsyati tasya vittād ghṛtena dyāvāpṛthivī
āpṛṇethāṃ devastvā savitā madhvānaktu supippalā oṣadhīskṛdhi dyāmagreṇāspṛkṣa āntarikṣaṃ madhyenāprāḥ pṛthivīmupareṇādṛm̐hīḥ ||
tā vāṃ vāstūnyuśmasi gamadhyai yaṃtra gāvo bhūriśṛṅgā ayāsaḥ |
atrāha tadurugāyasya viṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padamavabhāti bhūri ||
viṣṇoḥ karmāṇi paśyata yato vratāni paspaśe | indrasya yujyaḥ sakhā ||
brahmavaniṃ tvā kṣatravaniṃ devavaniṃ sajātavaniṃ rāyaspoṣavaniṃ paryūhāmi brahma dṛṃha kṣatraṃ dṛṃha prajāṃ dṛm̐ha rāyaspoṣaṃ dṛm̐ha ||
tadviṣṇoḥ paramaṃ padaṃ sadā paśyanti sūrayaḥ | divīva cakṣurātatam ||
parivīrasi pari tvā daivīrviśo vyayantāṃ parīmaṃ rāyo manuṣyamantarikṣasya sānūpeṣa dyāṃ te dhūmo gacchatvantarikṣamarciḥ pṛthivīṃ bhasma svāhā || 3 ||

iṣe tvopāvīrasyupa devāndaivīrviśaḥ prāgurvahnaya uśijo bṛhaspate dhārayā vasūni havyā te asvadan deva tvaṣṭarvasu raṇa revatī ramadhvamagnerjanitramasi vṛṣaṇau stha urvaśyasyāyurasi purūravā asi gāyatraṃ chando'nuprajāyasva traiṣṭubhaṃ chando'nuprajāyasva jāgataṃ chando'nuprajāyasva ||
bhavataṃ nassamanasau samokasā arepasau |
mā him̐siṣṭaṃ yajñapatiṃ mā yajñaṃ jātavedasau śivau bhavatamadya naḥ ||
agnā agniścarati praviṣṭa ṛṣīṇāṃ putro adhirāja eṣaḥ |
sa gāyatryā triṣṭubhā jagatyānuṣṭubhā devebhyo havyaṃ vahatu prajānan || 4 ||

devasya tvā savituḥ prasave'śvinorbāhubhyāṃ pūṣṇo hastābhyāmādada ṛtasya tvā devahaviḥ pāśena pratimuñcāmyamuṣmai juṣṭaṃ dharṣā mānuṣam̐ adbhyastvauṣadhībhyo'nu tvā mātā manyatāmanu pitānu bhrātā sagarbhyo'nu sakhā sayūthya āvaha devān devayate yajamānāyāpāṃ perurasi svāttam̐ havyaṃ devebhyo ghṛtavat saṃ te vāyuḥ prāṇena gacchatām̐ saṃ yajatrairaṅgāni saṃ yajñapatirāśiṣā || 5 ||

ghṛtenāktau paśuṃ trāyethāṃ revati predhā yajñapatimāviśoro antarikṣa sajūrdevena vātenāsya haviṣastmanā yaja samasya tanvā bhava varṣīyo varṣīyaso yajñaṃ yajñapatau dhā namasta ātānānarvā prehi ghṛtasya kulyāmanu saha prajayā saha rāyaspoṣeṇa devīrāpaśśuddhā yūyaṃ devān yūḍhvam̐ śuddhā vayaṃ pariviṣṭāḥ pariveṣṭāro vo bhūyāsma prāṇaṃ te mā hiṃsiṣaṃ cakṣuste mā him̐siṣam̐ śrotraṃ te mā him̐siṣaṃ vācaṃ te mā him̐siṣaṃ yatte krūrataraṃ yadāsthitaṃ tatta etena kalpatāṃ tatte mā him̐siṣaṃ caritrām̐ste mā hiṃsiṣaṃ nābhiṃ te mā him̐siṣaṃ meḍhraṃ te mā hisiṣaṃ pāyuṃ te mā him̐siṣaṃ devebhyaśśundhasva samadbhya oṣadhe trāyasvainam̐ svadhite mainam̐ hiṃsī rakṣasāṃ bhāgo'sīdamahaṃ rakṣo'vabādha idamahaṃ rakṣo'dhamaṃ tamo nayāmīṣe tvorje ghṛtena dyāvāpṛthivī prorṇuvāthāṃ devebhyaśśundhasva devebhyaśśumbhasvāmuṣmai tyā juṣṭāmurvantarikṣaṃ vīhi vāyo veṣṭokānāṃ pratyuṣṭaṃ rakṣaḥ pratyaṣṭārātirdevebhyassvāhā svāhā devebhyaḥ prayutā dveṣām̐si svāhordhvanabhasaṃ mārutaṃ gacchatam || 6 ||

reḍasyagnistvā śrīṇātvāpassamariṇan vātasya tvā dhrājyai pūṣṇo ram̐hyā ūṣmaṇo'vyathiṣyā apāmoṣadhīnāṃ rohiṣyā aindraḥ prāṇo aṅge aṅge nidīdhyadaindro vyāno aṅge aṅge nidīdhyadaindro'pāno aṅge aṅge nidadhyat ||
deva tvaṣṭarbhūri te sam̐ sametu salakṣma yadviṣurūpaṃ babhūva ||
devatrā yantamavase sakhāyamanu tvā mātā pitaro madantu |||
ghṛtaṃ vṛtapāvānaḥ pibata vasāṃ vasāpāvānaḥ pivatāntarikṣasya havirasi svāhā tvā vātāya diśaḥ pradiśa ādiśo vidiśa uddiśo diśassvāhā digbhyo namo digbhyaḥ || 7 ||

samudraṃ gaccha svāhāntarikṣaṃ gaccha svāhā devam̐ savitāraṃ gaccha svāhāhorātre gaccha svāhā mitrāvaruṇau gaccha svāhā dyāvāpṛthivī gaccha svāhā chando gaccha svāhā somaṃ gaccha svāhā yajñaṃ gaccha svāhā nabho gaccha svāhāgniṃ vaiśvānaraṃ gaccha svāhā mano me hārdiṃ yaccha putraṃ naptāramaśīyauṣadhībhyassvāhā || dhāmno dhāmno rājannito varuṇa no muñca yadāpo aghnyā varuṇeti śapāmahe tato varuṇa no muñca | |
uduttamaṃ varuṇa pāśamasmadavādhamaṃ vi madhyamaṃ śrathāya |
athā vayamāditya vrate tavanāgaso aditaye syāma ||
sumitrā ṇa āpa oṣadhayo bhavantu durmitrāstasmai santu yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ ca vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ||
dvīpe rājño varuṇasya gṛho hiraṇyayo mitaḥ ||
tato dhṛtavrato rājā dhāmno dhāmna iha muñcatu || 8 ||

haviṣmatīrimā āpo haviṣmān devo adhvaraḥ ||
haviṣmāṃ āvivāsati haviṣmānastu sūryaḥ ||
agnervo'pannagṛhasya sadasi sādayāmi sumnāyavassumnyāya sumnyaṃ dhattendrāgnyorbhāgadheyīsstha mitrāvaruṇayorbhāgadheyīsstha viśveṣāṃ devānāṃ bhāgadheyīsstha somasya vasatīvarīrhṛde tvā manase tvā dive tvā sūryāya tvordhvamimamadhvaraṃ divi deveṣu hotrā yaccha soma rājan viśvāḥ prajā upāvaroha viśvāstvā prajā upāvarohantu ||
śṛṇotvagnissamidhā havaṃ me śṛṇvantvāpo dhiṣaṇāśca devīḥ |
śṛṇotu grāvāṇo viduṣo nu yajñam̐ śṛṇotu devassavitā havaṃ me |
devīrāpo apāṃ napādya ūrmirhaviṣya indriyāvān madintamastaṃ devebhyo devatrā dāta śukrapebhyo yeṣāṃ bhāgasstha svāhā kāryrasi samudrasya vo'kṣityā unnaye ||
se vo dadhātu varuṇo mitro agnissamindrassaṃ bṛhaspatiḥ |
tvaṣṭā viṣṇuḥ prajayā saṃrarāṇā yajamānāya draviṇaṃ dadhātu ||
yathādhūrandhūro dhūrbhiḥ kalpantām ||
yamagne pṛtsu martyamavā vājeṣu yaṃ junāḥ | sa yantā śaśvatīriṣaḥ ||
svāhā vasūnāṃ rudrāṇāmādityānāṃ pannejanīsstha || vasavo rudrā ādityā etā vaḥ pannejanīssamudraṃ gandharveṣṭhāmanvātiṣṭhatha vātasya patmannīḍitā vāmī te saṃdṛśi viśvaṃ reto dhiṣīya tava vāmyanu saṃdṛśi || 9 ||

nigrābhyāsstha devaśrutaśśukrāśśukrabhṛta āyurme tarpayata prāṇaṃ me tarpayata vyānaṃ me tarpayatāpānaṃ me tarpayata cakṣurme tarpayata śrotraṃ me tarpayata vācaṃ me tarpayatātmānaṃ me tarpayata prajāṃ me tarpayata gṛhān me tarpayata paśūn me tarpayata gaṇān me tarpayata tṛptā mā tarpayata prītā mā prīṇīta te me mā vitṛṣan devasya tvā savituḥ prasave'śvinorbāhubhyāṃ pūṣṇo hastābhyāmādade grāvādhvarakṛddevebhya ūrdhvamimamadhvaraṃ kṛdhyuttamena pavinendrāya suṣuttamaṃ madhumantaṃ payasvantamindrāya tvābhimātighna indrāya tvā sahoda indrāya tvā vasumata indrāya tvā rudravata indrāya tvādityavate'gnaye tvā rāyaspoṣade viṣṇave tvā śyenāya tvā somabhṛte viṣṇave tvā yatte soma divi jyotiryat pṛthivyāṃ yadurā antarikṣe tenāsmai yajñapataya uru rāye kṛdhyadhi dhātre vocaśśvātrāsstha vṛtraturo rādhogūrtā amṛtasya patnīstā devīrdevatremaṃ yajñaṃ kṛtvopahūtāssomasya pibata mā bhairmā saṃvitthā dhiṣaṇe vīte vīḍayethāmūrjaṃ dadhathāmūrjamasmāsu dhattaṃ mā vām̐ him̐siṣaṃ mā yuvamasmān him̐siṣṭaṃ prāgapāgudagadharāktāstvā diśa adhāvantvamba nisvara samarirvidaḥ || 10 ||

iti śrīyajuṣi kāṭhake carakaśākhāyāmiṭhimikāyāṃ jyotirikaṃ nāma tṛtīyaṃ sthānakaṃ saṃpūrṇam ||3||
<4>
atha caturthaṃ sthānakam

grahāḥ ||
vācaspataye pavasva || vājin vṛṣṇo am̐śubhyāṃ gabhastipūto devo devānāṃ pavitramasi yeṣāṃ bhāgo'si madhumatīrna iṣaskṛdhi svaṃkṛto'syurvantarikṣaṃ vīhi viśvebhya indriyebhyo divyebhyaḥ pārthivebhyo manastvāṣṭu svāhā tvā subho sūryāya devebhyastvā marīcipebhyaḥ prāṇāya tvā vyānāya tvopayāmagṛhīto'si ||
antaryaccha maghavan pāhi somamuruṣya rāyassamiṣo yajasva |
antaste dyāvāpṛthivī dadhāmyantardadhāmyurvantarikṣam ||
sajoṣā devairavaraiḥ paraiścāntaryāme maghavan mādayasva ||
madhumatīrna īṣaskṛdhi svaṃkṛto'syurvantarikṣaṃ vīhi viśvebhya indriyebhyo divyebhyaḥ pārthivebhyo manastvāṣṭu svāhā tvā subho sūryāya devebhyastvā marīcipebhyo'pānāya tvā || 1 ||

ā vāyo bhūṣa śucipā upa nassahasraṃ te niyuto viśvavāra |
upo te andho madyamayāmi yato deva dadhiṣe pūrvapeyam ||
upayāmagṛhīto'si vāyave tvā ||
indravāyū ime sutā upa prayobhirāgatam ||
indavo vāgmuśanti hi ||
upayāmagṛhīto'si vāyava indravāyubhyāṃ tvaiṣa te yonissajoṣobhyāṃ tvā ||
ayaṃ vāṃ mitrāvaruṇā sutassoma ṛtāvṛdhā | mamediha śrutaṃ havam ||
upayāmagṛhīto'si mitrāvaruṇābhyāṃ tvaiṣa te yonirṛtāyubhyāṃ tvā |
yā vāṃ kaśā madhumatyaśvinā sūnṛtāvatī | tayā yajñaṃ mimikṣatam || upayāmagṛhīto'syaśvibhyāṃ tvaiṣa te yonirmādhvībhyāṃ tvopayāmagṛhīto'si vāyava indravāyubhyāṃ tvaiṣa te yonirviṣṇorurukrama upayāmagṛhīto'si mitrāvaruṇābhyāṃ tvaiṣa te yonirviṣṇorurukrama upayāmagṛhīto'syaśvibhyāṃ tvaiṣa te yonirviṣṇorurukrama taṃ rakṣasva mā tvā dabhan duścakṣāste māvakṣat || 2 ||

ayaṃ venaścodayat pṛśnigarbhā jyotirjarāyū rajaso vimānaḥ |
imamapām̐ saṃgame sūryasya śiśuṃ na viprā matibhī rihanti ||
upayāmagṛhīto'si śaṇḍāyendrāya tvā juṣṭaṃ gṛhṇāmyeṣa te yonirvīratāyai tvā |
taṃ pratnathā pūrvathā viśvathemathā jyeṣṭhatātiṃ barhiṣadam̐ svarvidam |
pratīcīnaṃ vṛjanaṃ dohase girā yā saṃjayantamadhi yāsu vardhase ||
upayāmagṛhīto'si markāya sūryāya tvā juṣṭaṃ gṛhṇāmyeṣa te yoniḥ prajābhyastvā || 3 ||

apanuttā śaṇḍāmarkau saha tena yaṃ dviṣmastutho'si janadhāyā devāstvā śukrapāḥ praṇayantu tutho'si janadhāyā devāstvā manthipāḥ praṇayantvāyussaṃdhattaṃ prāṇam̐ saṃdhattaṃ cakṣussaṃdhattam̐ śrotram̐ saṃdhattaṃ vācam̐ saṃdhattamanādhṛṣṭāsi jinvethām̐ svapatyā madhvā vītam̐ suvīrāḥ prajāḥ prajanayan parīhi suprajāḥ prajāḥ prajanayan parīhi ||
indreṇa manyunā yujā sāsahyāma pṛtanyataḥ | ghnanto vṛtrāṇyaprati |
saṃjagmānā divā pṛthivyā śukraśśukraśociṣā manthī manthiśociṣā śukro'si śukraśocirmanthyasi manthiśociracchinnasya te deva soma suvīryasya rāyaspoṣasya daditārassyāma nirastā śaṇḍāmarkau saha tena yaṃ dviṣmaśśukrasyādhiṣṭhānamasi manthino'dhiṣṭhānamasi ||
yā sam̐skṛtiḥ prathamā viśvakarmā yo madhyamo bṛhaspatiścikitvān |
yaḥ paramo varuṇo mitro agnistasmā indrāya sutamājuhota tasmai sūryāya sutamājuhota ||
svāhā tvā subho sūryāya devebhyastvā marīcipebhyaḥ || 4 ||

ye devā divyaikādaśa stha pṛthivyāmadhyekādaśa stha |
apsuṣado mahinaikādaśa stha te devāso yajñamimaṃ juṣadhvam || upayāmagṛhīto'syāgrāyaṇo jinva yajñaṃ jinva yajñapatimabhi saṃvanāni pāhi viṣṇustvāṃ pātu viśaṃ tvaṃ pāhīndriyeṇaiṣa te yonirviśvebhyastvā devebhyaḥ || upayāmagṛhīto 'sīndrāya tvā bṛhadvate vayasvata ukthāvyaṃ yatta indra bṛhadvayastasmai tvā viṣṇave tvaiṣa te yonirindrāya tvā ||
mūrdhānaṃ divo aratiṃ pṛthivyā vaiśvānaramṛta ā jātamagnim |
kaviṃ samrājamatithiṃ janānāmāsannā pātraṃ janayantu devāḥ ||
upayāmagṛhīto'si vaiśvānaro dhruvo'si dhuvakṣitirruyasavāṇāṃ dhruvatamo'cyutānāmacyutakṣittama eṣa te yonirvaiśvānarīya tvā || 5 ||

upayāmagṛhīto'si mitrāvaruṇābhyāṃ tvokthebhyastvokthāvyaṃ gṛhṇāmyeṣa te yonirmitrāvaruṇābhyāṃ tvā punarhavirasi devebhyastvā devāvyaṃ pṛṇacmi yajñasyāyuṣa upayāmagṛhīto'sīndrāya tvokthebhyastvokthāvyaṃ gṛhṇāmyeṣa te yonirindrāya tvā punarhavirasi devebhyastvā devāvyaṃ pṛṇacmi yajñasyāyuṣa upayāmagṛhīto'sīndrāgnibhyāṃ tvokthebhyastvokthāvyaṃ gṛhṇāmyeṣa te yonirindrāgnibhyāṃ tvā devebhyastvā devāvyaṃ pṛṇacmi yajñasyāyuṣa upayāmagṛhīto'sīndrāya tvendrāya tvokthebhyastvokthāvya gṛhṇāmyeṣa te yonirindrāya tvā devebhyastvā devāvyaṃ pṛṇacmi yajñasyāyuṣa upayāmagṛhīto'sīndrāvaruṇābhyāṃ tvokthebhyastvokthāvyaṃ gṛhṇāmyeṣa te yonirindrāvaruṇābhyāṃ tvā punarhavirasi devebhyastvā devāvyaṃ pṛṇacmi yajñasyāyuṣa upayāmagṛhīto'sīndrābṛhaspatibhyāṃ tvokthebhyastvokthāvyaṃ gṛhṇāmyeṣa te yonirindrābṛhaspatibhyāṃ tvā punarhavirasi devebhyastvā devāvyaṃ pṛṇacmi yajñasyāyuṣa upayāmagṛhīto'sīndrāviṣṇubhyāṃ tvokthebhyastvokthāvyaṃ gṛhrāmyeṣa te yonirindrāviṣṇubhyāṃ tvā punarhavirasi devebhyastvā devāvyaṃ pṛṇacmi yajñasyāyuṣe || 6 ||

upayāmagṛhīto'si madhave tvopayāmagṛhīto'si mādhavāya tvopayāmagṛhīto'si śukrāya tvopayāmagṛhīto'si śucaye tvopayāmagṛhīto'si nabhase tvopayāmagahīto'si nabhasyāya tvopayāmagṛhīto'sīṣāya tvopayāmagṛhīto'syūrjāya tyopayāmagṛhīto'si sahase tvopayāmagṛhīto'si sahasyāya tvopayāmagṛhīto'si tapase tvopayāmagṛhīto'si tapasyāya tvā ||
indrāgnī āgatam̐ sutaṃ gīrbhinabho vareṇyam | asya pātaṃ dhiyeṣitā || upayāmagṛhīto'sīndrāgnibhyāṃ tvaiṣa te yonirindrāgnibhyāṃ tvā |
omāsaścarṣaṇīdhṛto viśve devāsa āgata | dāśvām̐so dāśuṣassutam || upayāmagṛhīto'si viśvebhyastvā devebhya eṣa te yonirviśvebhyastvā devebhyaḥ || 7 ||

janiṣṭhā ugrassahase turāya mandraṃ ojiṣṭho bahulābhimānaḥ | |
avardhannindraṃ marutaścidatra mātā yadvīraṃ dadhanaddhaniṣṭhā |
upayāmagṛhīto'sīndrāya tvā marutvata eṣa te yonirindrāya tvā marutvate ||
indra marutva iha pāhi somaṃ yathā śāryāte apibassutasya ||
tava praṇītī tava śūra śarmannāvivāsanti kavayassuyajñāḥ ||
upayāmagṛhīto'sīndrāya tvā marutvata eṣa te yonirindrāya tvā marutvate ||
marutvām̐ indra vṛṣabho raṇāya pibā somamanuṣvadhaṃ madāya |
āsiñcasva jaṭhare madhva ūrmiṃ tvaṃ rājāsi pradivassutānām ||
upayāmagṛhīto'sīndrāya tvā marutvata eṣa te yonirindrāya tvā marutvate ||
marutvantaṃ vṛṣabhaṃ vāvṛdhānamakavāriṃ divyaṃ śāsamindram ||
viśvāsāhamavase nūtanāyogram̐ sahodāmiha taṃ huvema ||
upayārmagṛhīto'sīndrāya tvā marutvata eṣa te yonirindrāya tvā marutvate ||
mahām̐ indro ya ojasā parjanyo vṛṣṭimām̐ iva | stomairvatsasya vāvṛdhe | upayāmagṛhīto'si mahendrāya tvaiṣa te yonirmahendrāya tvā ||
mahām̐ indro nṛvadā carṣaṇiprā uta dvibarhā aminassahobhiḥ |
asmadryagvāvṛdhe vīryāyoruḥ pṛthussukṛtaḥ kartṛbhirbhṛt ||
upayāmagṛhīto'si mahendrāya tvaiṣa te yonirmahendrāya tvā || 8 ||

udu tyaṃ jātavedasaṃ devaṃ vahanti ketavaḥ | dṛśe viśvāya sūryam ||
citraṃ devānāmudagādanīkaṃ cakṣurmitrasya varuṇāsyāgneḥ |
āprā dyāvāpṛthivī antarikṣam̐ sūrya ātmā jagatastasthuṣaśca ||
dyāṃ gaccha svargaccha rūpaṃ vo rūpeṇābhyāṃgāṃ vayasā vayastutho vo viśvavedā vibhajatu varṣiṣṭhe'dhi nāka etatte agne rādha eti somacyutaṃ tanmitrasya pathā nayartasya pathā preta candradakṣiṇā brāhmaṇamadyardhyāsaṃ pitṛmantaṃ paitṛmatyamṛṣimārṣeyam̐ sudhātudakṣiṇam ||
ayaṃ no agnirvarivaḥ kṛṇotvayaṃ mṛdhaḥ puraetu prabhindan |
ayam̐ śatrūñjayatu jarhṛṣāṇo'yaṃ vājaṃ jayatu vājasātau ||
vaneṣu vyantarikṣaṃ tatāna vājamarvatsu payo aghnyāsu |
hṛtsu kratuṃ varuṇo vikṣvagniṃ divi sūryamadadhāt somamadrau ||
vi svaḥ paśya vyantarikṣaṃ yatasva sadasyairasmadrātā madhumatī devatrā gaccha pradātāramāviśānavahāyāsmān devi dakṣiṇe devayānena pathā yatī sukṛtāṃ loke sīda tannau sam̐skṛtam || 9||

kadā cana starīrasi nendra saścasi dāśuṣe |
upopennu maghavan bhūya innu te dānaṃ devasya pṛcyate || upayāmagṛhīto'syādityebhyastvā ||
kadā cana prayucchasyubhe nipāsi janmanī |
turīyāditya savanaṃ ta indriyamātasthā amṛtaṃ divi || upayāmagṛhīto'syādityebhyastvā ||
yajño devānāṃ pratyeti sumnamādityāso bhavatā mṛḍayantaḥ ||
ā vo'rvācī sumatirvavṛtyādam̐hościdyā varivovittarāsat || upayāmagṛhīto'syādityebhyastvā || vivasva ādityaiṣa te somapīthastasmin mandasva || yā divyā vṛṣṭistayā tvā śrīṇāmi ||
adabdhebhissavitaḥ pāyubhiṣṭvam̐ śivebhiradya paripāhi no gayam || hiraṇyajihvassuvitāya navyase rakṣā mākirno aghaśam̐sa īśata ||
upayāmagṛhīto'si devāya tvā savitra upayāmagṛhīto'si suśarmāsi supratiṣṭhāno bṛhadukṣe nama eṣa te yonirviśvebhyastvā devebhyaḥ || 10 ||

upayāmagṛhīto'si bṛhaspatisutasya inda indriyāvataḥ patnīvato grahamadhyāsamagnā3 i patnīvā3ntsajūstvaṣṭrā somaṃ pibopayāmagṛhīto'si harirasi hāriyojano haryossthātā haribhyāṃ tvā stutastomasya te deva soma śastokthasyeṣṭayajuṣo harivato grahamṛdhyāsam̐ hayām̐sstha harivato dhānāssahasomā indrasya||
agna āyūm̐ṣi pavasa āsuvorjamiṣaṃ ca naḥ | āre bādhasva ducchanām || upayāmagṛhīto'syagnaye tvā tejasvata eṣa te yoniragnaye tvā tejasvate ||
ojastadasya titviṣa ubhe yatsamavartayat | indraścarmeva rodasī || upayāmagṛhīto'sīndrāya tvaujasvata eṣa te yonirindrāya tvaujasvate ||
adṛśrannasya ketavo vi raśmayo janām̐ anu | bhrājanto agnayo yathā || upayāmagṛhīto'si sūryāya tvā bhrājasvata eṣa te yonissūryāya tvā bhrājasvate ||
indramiddharī vahato'pratidhṛṣṭaśavasam | ṛṣīṇāṃ ca stutīrupa yajñaṃ ca mānuṣāṇām || upayāmāhīto'sīndrāya tvā harivata eṣa te yonirindrāya tvā harivate || 11 ||

dhātā rātissavitedaṃ juṣantāṃ prajāpatirvaruṇo mitro agniḥ |
tvaṣṭā viṣṇuḥ prajayā saṃrarāṇā yajamānāya draviṇaṃ dadhātu ||
samindra no manasā neṣi gobhissam̐ sūribhirharivassam̐ svastyā |
saṃ brahmaṇā devakṛtaṃ yadasti saṃ devānām̐ sumatyā yajñiyānām ||
saṃ varcasā payasā saṃ tanūbhiraganmahi manasā sam̐ śivena |
tvaṣṭā sudatro vidadhātu rāyo'nu no mārṣṭu tanvo yadviliṣṭam ||
sugā vo devāssadanedamastu ya ājagma savanedaṃ juṣāṇāḥ |
jakṣivām̐saḥ papivām̐saśca viśve'sme dhatta vasavo vasūni ||
yānāvaha uśato deva devām̐stān preraya sve agne sadhasthe |
vahamānā bharamāṇā havīm̐ṣyasuṃ gharmaṃ divamātiṣṭhatānu ||
yadadya tvā prayati yajñe asminagne hotāramavṛṇīmahīha ||
ṛdhagayāḍṛdhagutāśamiṣṭa vidvān prajānannupayāhi yajñam ||
yajña yajñaṃ gaccha yajñapatiṃ gaccha svaṃ yoniṃ gaccha svāhaiṣa te yajño yajñapate sahasūktavākassuvorassvāhā devā gātuvido gātuṃ vittvā gātumita manasaspata imaṃ devayajñam̐ svāhā vāci svāhā vāte dhāḥ || 12 ||

urum̐ hi rājā varuṇaścakāra sūryāya panthāmanvetavā u ||
apade pādā pratidhātave'karutāpavaktā hṛdayāvidhaścit ||
śataṃ te rājan bhiṣajassahasramurvī gabhīrā sumatiṣṭe astu |
āre bādhasva nirṛtiṃ parācaiḥ kṛtaṃ cidenaḥ pramumugdhyasmāt ||
agneranīkamapa āviveśāpāṃ napāt pratirakṣannasuryam |
dame dame samidhaṃ yakṣyagne prati te jihvā ghṛtamuccaraṇyat ||
samudre te hṛdayamapsvantassaṃ tvā viśantvoṣadhīrutāpaḥ |
yajñasya tvā yajñapate saha sūktavāke namovāke vidhema ||
svāhāvabhṛtha nicuṅkuṇa nicerurasi nicuṅkuṇo'va no devairdevakṛtameno yakṣyava martyairmartyakṛtamurorā no deva riṣaspāhi devīrāpa eṣa vo garbhastaṃ vassuprītaṃ subhṛtamabhārṣaṃ deveṣu nassukṛto brūtābhiṣṭhito varuṇasya pāśo'vahato varuṇasya pāśo'psu dhautasya te deva soma nṛbhisstutasya yo bhakṣo gosaniryo'śvasanistasya ta upahūta upahūtasya bhakṣaṃ kṛṇomi pratyasto varuṇasya pāśo namo varuṇasya pāśāya ||
udeta prajāmāyurvarco dadhānā adha syāma surabhayo gṛheṣu ||
gāyatrī chandām̐syanu sam̐rabhantāmasmān rāya urta yajñassacantām ||
suprītaśśiśura āviveśa | unnetarvasyo'bhyunnayā naḥ || edho'syedhiṣīmahi samidasi samedhiṣīmahi tejo'si tejo mayi dhehi |||
apo adyānvacāriṣaṃ rasena samaganmahi | payasvānagna āgamaṃ taṃ mā sam̐sṛja varcasā || saṃ māgne varcasā sṛja prajayā ca dhanena ca |
vidyurme asya devā indro vidyāda saharṣibhiḥ || 13 ||

mamāgne varco vihaveṣvastu vayaṃ tvendhānāstanvaṃ puṣema |
mahyaṃ namantāṃ pradiśaścatasrastvayādhyakṣeṇa pṛtanā jayema ||
agne vratapate vratamālabhe tatte prabravīmi tacchakeyaṃ tenardhyāsam ||
agnirhotopa tam̐ huve devān yajñiyāniha yān yajāmahai |
ā devā yantu sumanasyamānā vyantu devā haviṣo me asya ||
yunajmi tvā brahmaṇā daivyena havyāyāsmai voḍhavai jātavedaḥ ||
indhānātvā suprajasassuvīrā jyogjīvema balihṛto vayaṃ te ||
aṅgiraso māsya yajñasya prathamānuvākairavantu || samiddho agnirāhutassvāhākṛtaḥ pipartu naḥ || mano'si prājāpatyaṃ manasā mā bhūtenāviśa vāgasyaindrī sapatnakṣayaṇī vācāmindriyeṇāviśa devāḥ pitaraḥ pitaro devā yo'smi sa sanyaje tadvaḥ prabravīmi tasya me vitta svaṃ ma iṣṭamastu śunam̐ śāntam̐ svaṃ kṛtaṃ vasantamṛtūnāṃ prīṇāmi sa mā prītaḥ prīṇātu vasantasyāhaṃ devayajyayorjasvān payasvān bhūyāsaṃ grīṣmamṛtūnāṃ prīṇāmi sa mā prītaḥ prīṇātu grīṣmasyāhaṃ devayajyayaujasvaॉstejasvāna bhūyāsaṃ varṣāṛtūnāṃ prīṇāmi tā mā prītāḥ prīṇantu varṣāṇāmahaṃ devayajyayā puṣṭimān paśumān bhūyāsam̐ śaradamṛtūnāṃ prīṇāmi sā mā prītā prīṇātu śarado'haṃ devayajyayānnavān varcasvān bhūyāsam̐ hemantamṛtūnāṃ prīṇāmi sa mā prītaḥ prīṇātu hemantasyāhaṃ devayajyayā sahasvān vīryāvān bhūyāsam || 14 ||

indrāgnī rocanā divaḥ pari vājeṣu bhūṣathaḥ | tadvāṃ ceti pra vīryam ||
śnathadvṛtramuta sanoti vājamindrā yo agnī sahurī saparyāt |
irajyantā vasavyasya bhūressahastamā sahasā vājayantā ||
yā vām̐ santi puruspṛho niyuto dāśuṣe narā | indrāgnī tābhirāgatam ||
tā yodhiṣṭamabhi gā indra nūnamapassvaruṣaso agna ūḍhāḥ ||
diśassvaruṣasa indra citrā apo gā agne yuvase niyutvān ||
tā vāmeṣe rathānāmindrāgnī havāmahe | patī turasya rādhaso vidvām̐sā girvaṇastamā ||
aśravam̐ hi bhūridāvattarā vāṃ vijāmāturuta vā ghā syālāt |
adhā somasya prayatī yuvābhyāmindrāgnī stomaṃ janayāmi navyam ||
indrāgnī navatiṃ puro dāsapatnīradhūnutam | sākamekena karmaṇā ||
ā vṛtrahaṇā vṛtrahabhiśśuṣmairindra yātaṃ namobhiragne arvāk ||
yuvaṃ rādhobhirakavebhirindrāgne asme bhavatamuttamebhiḥ ||
ugrā vighaninā mṛdha indrāgrī havāmahe | tā no mṛḍāta īdṛśe ||
pra carṣaṇibhyaḥ pṛtanāhaveṣu pra pṛthivyā riricāthe divaśca |
pra sindhubhyaḥ pra giribhyo mahitvā prendrāgnī viśvā bhuvanātyanyā ||
gomaddhiraṇyavadvasu yadvāmaśvāvadīmahe | indrāgnī tadvanemahi ||
gīrbhirvipraḥ pramatimicchamāna īṭṭe rayiṃ yaśasaṃ pūrvabhājam ||
indrāgnī vṛtrahaṇā suvajrā pra no navyebhistirataṃ deṣṇaiḥ ||
pūṣā gā anvetu naḥ pūṣā rakṣatvarvataḥ | pūṣā vājaṃ sanotu naḥ ||
śukraṃ te anyadyajataṃ te anyadviṣurūpe ahanī dyaurivāsi |
viśvā hi māyā avasi svadhāvo bhadrā te pūṣanniha rātirastu |
kṣetrasya patinā vayam̐ hiteneva jayāmasi | gāmaśvaṃ poṣayitnvā sa no mṛḍātīdṛśe ||
kṣetrasya pate madhumantamūrmiṃ dhenuriva payo asmāsu dhukṣva |
madhuścyutaṃ ghṛtamiva supūtamṛtasya naḥ patayo mṛḍayantu || 15 ||

agnāviṣṇū mahi tadvāṃ mahitvaṃ pātho ghṛtasya guhyāni nāma |
dame dame sapta ratnā dadhānā prati vāṃ jihvā ghṛtamuccaraṇyat ||
agnāviṣṇū mahi dhāma priyaṃ vāṃ vītho ghṛtasya guhyā juṣāṇā |
dame dame suṣṭutyā vāvṛdhānānu vāṃ jihvā ghṛtamācaraṇyat ||
agnirmukhaṃ prathamo devatānām̐ saṃgatānāmuttamo viṣṇurāsīt |
yajamānāya parigṛhya devān dīkṣāyedaṃ havirāgacchataṃ naḥ ||
iyamadadādrabhasamṛṇacyutaṃ divodāsaṃ vadhryaśvāya dāśuṣe ||
yā śaśvantamācakhādāvasaṃ paṇiṃ tā te dātrāṇi taviṣā sarasvati ||
iyaṃ śuṣmebhirvisakhā ivārujat sānu girīṇāṃ taviṣebhirūrmibhiḥ |
pārāvataghnīmavase suvṛktibhissarasvatīmāvivāsema dhītibhiḥ |
janāya cidya īvata u lokaṃ bṛhaspatirdevahūtau cakāra |
ghnan vṛtrāṇi vi puro dardarīti jayañchatrūm̐ramitrān pṛtsu sāhan |
bṛhaspatissamajayadvasūni maho vrajān gomato deva eṣaḥ |
apaḥ siṣāsan svarapratīto bṛhaspatirhantyamitramarkaiḥ ||
pāvakā nassarasvatī vājebhirvājinīvatī | yajñaṃ vaṣṭu dhiyāvasuḥ ||
imā juhvānā yuṣmadā namobhiḥ prati stomam̐ sarasvati juṣasva |
tava śarman priyatame dadhānā upa stheyāma śaraṇaṃ na vṛkṣam ||
ā no divo bṛhataḥ parvatādā sarasvatī yajatā gantu yajñam |
havaṃ devī jujuṣāṇā ghṛtācī śagmāṃ no vācamuśatī śṛṇotu ||
vṛṣabhaṃ carṣaṇīnāṃ viśvarūpamadābhyam | bṛhaspatiṃ vareṇyam ||
aṃdevena manasā yo riṣaṇyati śāsāmugro manyamāno jighām̐sati |
bṛhaspate mā praṇaktasya no vadho nikarma manyuṃ durevasya śardhataḥ ||
bṛhaspate juṣasva no havyāni viśvadevya | rāsva ratnāni dāśuṣe ||
bṛhaspate ati yadaryo arhād dyumadvibhāti kratumajjaneṣu |
yaddīdayacchavasartaprajāta tadasmāsu draviṇaṃ dhehi citram ||
ā no mitrāvaruṇā ghṛtairgavyūtimukṣatam | madhvā rajām̐si sukratū ||
pra bāhavā sisṛtaṃ jīvase na ā no gavyūtimukṣataṃ ghṛtena |
ā no jane śravayataṃ yuvānā śrutaṃ me mitrāvaruṇā havemā |
hiraṇyagarbhaḥ ||
yaḥ prāṇato nimiṣataśca rājā patirviśvasya jagato babhūva ||
ya īśe asya dvipadaścatuṣpadaḥ kasmai devāya haviṣā vidhema ||
agnīṣomā imeṃ su me śṛṇutaṃ vṛṣaṇā havam | prati sūktāni haryataṃ bhavataṃ dāśuṣe mayaḥ||
agnīṣomā savedasā sahūtī vanataṃ giraḥ | saṃ devatrā babhūvathuḥ ||
yuvametāni divi rocanānyagniśca soma sakratū adhattam |
yuvam̐ sindhūṃrabhiśasteravadyādagnīṣomā amuñcataṃ gṛbhītān ||
ānyaṃ divo mātariśvā jabhārāmathnādanyaṃ pari śyeno adreḥ |
agnīṣomā brahmaṇā vāvṛdhānoruṃ yajñāya cakrathuru lokam ||
vaiśvānaro na ūtaya ā prayātu parāvataḥ | agnirukthena vāhasā ||
tvadvipro jāyate vājyagne tvadvīrāso abhimātiṣāhaḥ |
vaiśvānara tvamasmāsu dhehi vasūni rājan spṛhayāyyāṇi |
ṛtāvānaṃ vaiśvānaramṛtasya jyotiṣaspatim | ajasraṃ gharmamīmahe ||
vaiśvānarasya sumatau syāma rājā hi kaṃ bhuvanānāmabhiśrīḥ |
ito jāto viśvamidaṃ vicaṣṭe vaiśvānaro yatate sūryeṇa ||
pṛṣṭo divi pṛṣṭo agniḥ pṛthivyāṃ pṛṣṭo viśvā auṣadhīrāviveśa |
vaiśvānarassahasā pṛṣṭo agnissa no divā sa riṣaḥ pātu naktam ||
asmākamagne maghavatsu dhārayānāmi kṣatramajaraṃ suvīram ||
vayaṃ jayema śatinam̐ sahasriṇaṃ vaiśvānara vājamagne tavotibhiḥ ||
imaṃ me varuṇa śrudhī havamadyā ca mṛḍaya | tvāmavasyurācake ||
tattvā yāmi brahmaṇā vandamānastadāśāste yajamāno havirbhiḥ |
aheḍamāno varuṇeha bodhyuruśam̐sa mā na āyuḥ pramoṣīḥ ||
śatena pāśairvaruṇābhidhehi mā te mocyanṛtavāṅ nṛcakṣaḥ ||
āstāṃ jalma udaram̐ sram̐sayitvā kośa ivābandhraḥ parikṛtyamānaḥ ||
dhīrā tvasya mahinā janūm̐ṣi vi yastastambha rodasī cidurvī |
pra nākamṛṣvaṃ nunude bṛhantaṃ dvitā nakṣatraṃ paprathacca bhūma || 16 ||

atha pañcamaṃ sthānakam ||

yajamānam |
agnīṣomābhyāṃ yajñaścakṣuṣmām̐stayorahaṃ devayajyayā cakṣuṣā cakṣuṣmān bhūyāsamagnirannasyānnapatistasyāhaṃ devayajyayānnasyānnapatirbhūyāsaṃ dabdhirasyadabdho bhūyāsamamuṃ dabheyamagnīṣomau vṛtrahaṇau tayorahaṃ devayajyayā vṛtrahā bhūyāsamindrāgnyorahaṃ devayajyayaujasvān vīryāvān bhūyāsamindrasyāhaṃ devayajyayendriyāvān bhūyāsaṃ mahendrasyāhaṃ devayajyayā jehamānaṃ bhūmānaṃ gameyamindrasyāhaṃ vimṛdhasya devayajyayāsapatno bhūyāsamindrasyāhamindriyāvato devayajyayā paśumān bhūyāsam|| sarasvatyā ahaṃ devayajyayā vācamannādyaṃ puṣeyaṃ pūṣṇo'haṃ devayajyayā puṣṭimān paśumān bhūyāsamadityā ahaṃ devayajyayā pratiṣṭhāṃ gameyaṃ viśveṣāmahaṃ devānāṃ devayajyayā prāṇānām̐ sāyujyaṃ gameyaṃ dyāvāpṛthivyorahaṃ devayajyayā prajaniṣeyaṃ prajayā paśubhiragneraham̐ sviṣṭakṛto devayajyayāyuḥ pratiṣṭhāṃ gameyam ||
atītapadyajñe yajñamayāḍ devām̐ araṃkṛtaḥ | somaste mitro aryamā rāyaspoṣaṃ dadhātana ||1||

bṛhato mā vājena vājaya vāmadevasya mā vājena vājaya rathantarasya mā vājeya vājaya juṣṭe juṣṭiṃ te gameyamupahūta upahavaṃ te'śīya suhavāmehi saha prajayā saha rāyaspoṣeṇa ||
asmāsvindra indriyaṃ dadhātvasmān rāyo maghavānassacantām ||
asmākam̐ santvāśiṣaḥ ||
āśīrma ūrjamuta suprajāstvamiṣaṃ dadhātu draviṇem̐ savarcasam |
saṃjayan kṣetrāṇi sahasāhamindra kurvāṇo anyām̐ adharān sapatnān ||
brahma tejo me pinvasva kṣatramojo me pinvasva prajāṃ vṛṣṭiṃ me pinvasva paśūn viśaṃ me pinvasveṣamūrjaṃ me pinvasva ||
mayi tyadindriyaṃ mahanmayi dyumna uta kratuḥ ||
triśuggharmassadaminme vibhāti virāḍa़ jyotiṣā saha ||
tasya dohamaśīya bradhna pinvasva kalpantāṃ diśo yajamānasyāyuṣe dadato me mā kṣāyi kurvato me mopadasadbhajatāṃ bhāgī mābhāgo bhakta ye brāhmaṇāssomyāsteṣāmidam̐ havirnāsomyasyāpyasti nirbhakto yaṃ dviṣmaḥ || 2 ||

devasyāhaṃ barhiṣo devayajyayā prajāvān bhūyāsaṃ devasyāhaṃ narāśam̐sasya devayajyayā paśumān bhūyāsaṃ devasyāhamagnessviṣṭakṛto devayajyayāyuḥ pratiṣṭhāṃ gameyam ||
ā māśiṣaḥ dohakāmā indravanto havāmahe | dhukṣīmahi prajāmiṣam ||
sā me satyāśīrdevān gamyājjuṣṭājjuṣṭatarā panyāt panyatarāheḍatā manasā devān gaccha yo devayānaḥ panthāstena devān gaccha yajño devān gacchatu yajño devān gamyādado mā gacchatu || rohitena tvāgnirdevatāṃ gamayatu haribhyāṃ tvendro devatāṃ gamayatvetaśena tvā sūryo devatāṃ gamayatu yajña yajñaṃ gaccha yajñapatiṃ gaccha yajñasyāyurasi yajño ma āyurdadhātu ||
vi te muñcāmi raśanāṃ vi raśmīn vi yoktrāṇi paricartanāni ||
dattvāyāsmabhyaṃ draviṇeha bhadraṃ pra mā brūtāddhavirdā devatābhyaḥ ||
viṣṇośca yajñasyāntastayorahaṃ devayajyayāyuḥ pratiṣṭhāṃ gameyam || 3 ||

iṣṭo yajño bhṛgubhirdraviṇodā yatibhirāśīrvām̐ atharvabhiḥ|| tasya yajñasyeṣṭasya sviṣṭasya draviṇamāgacchatvaṅgiraso me'sya yajñasya prātaranuvākairahauṣurvasuryajño vasumān yajño vasu vasīya vasumanto bhūyāsma tasya yajñasya vasorvasumato vasu māgacchatu somo retodhāstasyāhaṃ devayajyayā suretodhā reto dhiṣīya tvaṣṭā rūpāṇāṃ vikartā tasyāhaṃ devayajyayā viśvarūpaṃ priyaṃ puṣeyaṃ devānāṃ patnīragnirgṛhapatirmithunaṃ yajamānasya tayorahaṃ devayajyayā mithunena prajaniṣīyāyuṣe varcase rāyaspoṣāya suprajāstvāya||
saṃ patnī patyā sukṛteṣu gacchatāṃ yajñasya yuktau dhuryā abhūtām |
āprīṇāno vijahatā arātiṃ dive jyotiruttamamārabhethām ||
svāhā svāheṣṭibhyo vaṣaḍaniṣṭebhyo bhiṣajau sviṣṭyai svāhā niṣkṛtirduriṣṭayai svāhā || devebhyastanūbhyassvāhā ||
ayāścāgne'syanabhiśastiśca satyamittvamayā asi ||
ayāssanmanasā kṛto'yāssanhavyamūhiṣe'yā no dhehi bheṣajam ||
svāhā sarasvatyai veśabhaginyai svāhā || yā sarasvatī veśabhaginī tasyā no rāsva tasyāste bhaktivāno bhūyāsma yajñasya tvā pramayābhimayonmayā pratimayā parigṛhṇāmi vedo'si vittirasi vedase tvā vedo me vinda videya |||
ghṛtavantaṃ kulāyinaṃ rāyaspoṣam̐ sahasriṇam | vedo vājaṃ dadātu me vedo vīraṃ dadātu me || vṛṣā vṛṣaṇvatībhyo vedapatnībhyo bhava || 4 ||

prajāpaterbhāgo'syūrjasvān payasvānakṣito'syakṣityai tvakṣito nāmāsi mā me kṣeṣṭhāḥ prāṇāpānau me pāhi samānavyānau me pāhyudānarūpe me pāhyūrgasyūrjaṃ mayi dhehyā mā gamyāḥ pūrṇamasi pūrṇaṃ me bhūyāssadasi sanme bhūyāssarvamasi sarvaṃ me bhūyā akṣitirasi mā me kṣeṣṭhāḥ prācyā diśā devā ṛtvijo mārjayantāṃ dakṣiṇayā diśā māsāḥ pitaro mārjayantāṃ pratīcyā diśā gṛhāḥ paśavo mārjayantāmudīcyā diśāpa oṣadhayo vanaspatayo mārjayantāmūrdhvayā diśā yajñassaṃvatsaro mārjayantām || pṛthivīṃ viṣṇurvyakram̐sta gāyatreṇa cchandasā nirbhakto yaṃ dviṣmo'ntarikṣaṃ viṣṇurvyakram̐sta traiṣṭubhena cchandasā nirbhakto yaṃ dviṣmo divaṃ viṣṇurvyakram̐sta jāgatena cchandasā nirbhakto yaṃ dviṣmo diśo viṣṇurvyakram̐stānuṣṭubhena cchandasā nirbhakto yaṃ dviṣmo'ganma svassaṃ jyotiṣābhūma sūryasyāvṛtamanvāvarta idamahaṃ yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ ca vayaṃ dviṣmastasya prāṇaṃ niveṣṭayāmi tejo'si tejo mayi dhehi samahaṃ prajayā saṃ mayā prajā samahaṃ rāyaspoṣeṇa saṃ mayā rāyaspoṣo'gnestejasā tejasvī bhūyāsaṃ vāyorāyuṣāyuṣmān bhūyāsam̐ sūryasya varcasā varcasvī bhūyāsamindrasyendriyeṇendriyāvān bhūyāsaṃ dhātā me dhāmnā sudhāṃ dadhātu prajāpateḥ prajayā prajāvān bhūyāsamagne gṛhapate sugṛhapatirahaṃ tvayā gṛhapatinā bhūyāsam̐ sugṛhapatirmayā tvaṃ gṛhapatinā bhūyā asthūri ṇau gārhapatyaṃ dīdāyacchatam̐ himādvāyū ||5||

devāñjanamagan yajñastato mā yajñasyāśīrāgacchatu pitṝn sarpān gandharvānapa oṣadhīḥ pañca janāñjanamagan yajñastato mā yajñasyāśīrāgacchatu pañcānāṃ tvā vātānāṃ dhartrāyāgṛhṇāmi pañcānāṃ tvā salilānāṃ dhartrāyāgṛhṇāmi pañcānāṃ tvā pṛṣṭhānāṃ dhartrāyāgṛhṇāmi pañcānāṃ tvā diśāṃ dhartrāyāgṛhṇāmi pañcānāṃ tvā pañcajanānāṃ dhartrāyāgṛhṇāmi, bhūrasmākam̐ havyaṃ devānāmāśiṣo yajamānasya carostvā pañcabilasya dhartrāyāgṛhṇāmi dhāmāsi priyaṃ devānāmanādhṛṣṭaṃ devayajanaṃ devatābhistvā devatābhyo gṛhṇāmi ||
ye devā yajñahano yajñamuṣaḥ pṛthivyāmadhyāsate |
agnirmā tebhyo rakṣatu gacchema sukṛto vayam ||
yāste rātrayassavitardevayānīssahasrayajñamabhisaṃbabhūvuḥ ||
gṛhaiśca sarvaiḥ prajayā nvagre svo ruhāṇāstaratā rajām̐si |
ye devā yajñahano yajñamuṣo'ntarikṣe'dhyāsate ||
vāyurmā tebhyo rakṣatu gacchema sukṛto vayam ||
āganma mitrāvaruṇā vareṇyaṃ rātrīṇāṃ bhāgo yuvayoryo asti ||
nākaṃ gṛhṇānāssukṛtasya loke tṛtīye pṛṣṭhe adhi rocane divaḥ ||
ye devā yajñahano yajñamuṣo divyadhyāsate ||
sūryo mā tebhyo rakṣatu gacchema sukṛto vayam ||
yenendrāya samabharan payām̐syuttamena haviṣā jātavedaḥ |
tenāgne tvamuta vardhayemam̐ sujātānām̐ śreṣṭhya ādhehyenam ||
gomām̐ agne'vimām̐ aśvī yajño nṛvatsakhā sadamidapramṛṣyaḥ |
iḍāvām̐ eṣo asura prajāvān dīrgho rayiḥ pṛthubudhnassabhāvān |
agne vratapate vratamacāriṣaṃ tadaśakaṃ tatte prabūmastanno gopāya || 6 ||

iti śrīyajuṣi kāṭhake carakaśākhāyāmiṭhimikāṃyā yajamānaṃ nāma pañcamaṃ sthānakaṃ saṃpūrṇam || 5 ||
<6>
atha ṣaṣṭhaṃ sthānakam |

agnihotrabrāhmaṇam |
prajāpatirvā idamāsīt tasmādagniradhyasṛjyata so'sya mūrdhna ūrdhva udadravat tasya yallohitamāsīt tadapāmṛṣṭa tadbhūmyāṃ nyamārṭ tata udumbaro'jāyata tasmāt sa lohitaṃ pacyate sa prāṅ prādravat tam̐ svā vāgaiṭṭa juhudhīti sa itaḥ paryamṛṣṭa tat svāhetyajuhot tasmādeṣaivamāhutissvā hyenaṃ vāgaiṭṭa tasmānna lalāṭe lomāsti na pāṇyostasmādaratnimātrī srugbhavati cakṣurvāva sa tat svamajuhodamumevādityamagnau jyotirjyotiragnā iti jyotireva jyotiṣyajuhodbrahmājuhot satyamajuhodamumevaṃ tadādityama juhodeṣa hyevāgnihotram̐ śvaśśvo vasīyān bhavati yasyaivamagnihotram̐ hūyate || na vai purāhorātre āstāṃ te etayāhutyobhe sahasṛjyetāṃ yat sāyaṃ juhoti tena bhrātṛvyāya parācīṃ vivāsayati yat prātastenātmane pratīcīmagnervai guptyā agnihotram̐ hūyate yat sāyaṃ juhoti tenainaṃ rātryai ramayati yat prātastenāhna eṣā vā agnā āhutiḥ prathamā hutā yadagnihotraṃ tasmādagnau sarvā āhutayo hūyante'thaivāgnihotramucyate'gnihotram̐ ha vai nāma nāsmādanyassamāneṣu vasīyān bhavati yasyaivamagnihotram̐ hūyate || 1 ||

sa nāramata sa prādravat tasminnajuhot tasyā āhutyāḥ puruṣo'jāyata sa nāramata sa prādravat tasminnajuhot tasyā āhutyā aśvo'jāyata tasmāt puruṣaścāśvaśca naktaṃ pratyañcau na su vijñāyete iva sa nāramata sa prādravat tasminnajuhot tasyā āhutyā gaurajāyatāvirajāyatājājāyata yavo'jāyata vrīhirajāyataitābhyo saptabhya āhutibhyassapteme grāmyāḥ paśavo'sṛjyanta sa nāramata se prādravat tasminnajuhot tasyā āhutyā vasanto'jāyata grīṣmo'jāyata varṣā ajāyanta śaradajāyata hemanta'jāyata sa trayodaśyāmāhutyāmudyatāyāmabibhet taṃ pratyaṅṅupāpadyata taṃ pāṇitaḥ praviśat tasmāt pāṇyorloma nāstyagnidhānam̐ hyetat tasmāddhastābhyāmagnirmathyamāno jāyate || tasmāddhanyamānau hastau pratigṛhṇātyagnā evaṃ tat trāṇamicchate'gniṃ paśavo'nūpatiṣṭhante paśūnagniḥ prainaṃ paśavo viśanti pra paśūn viśati ya evaṃ veda tāmāhutimapsu prāsiñcat tataśśiśiramajāyata tasmāt tat krūratamaṃ nahi taddhutānnāhutādajāyata sahutirapo dagdhumupākrāmata tata āpo'gnaye vajro'bhavam̐stasmāt prokṣannagniṃ na prāpayedyat prāpayeddhatena yajeta tāmāhutimoṣadhīṣu nyadadhāt tasmādoṣadhayo'nabhyaktā rebhanta auṣadhibhyo'dhi paśavaḥ paśubhyo'dhi prajāstadetacchiśira ājyaṃ śyāyatyagnaye tasmācchiśire kakṣa upādutyaḥ priyameva dhāmāgnaye prādāt so'smā annādyaṃ prayacchatyannādo bhavati ya evaṃ veda || 2 ||

samāne vai yonā āstām̐ sūryaścāgniśca tatassūrya ūrdhva udadravat tasya retaḥ parāpatat tadagniryoninopāgṛhṇādayasā tadakrūḍayat tat krūḍyamānaṃ gavi nyadadhāt tadidaṃ payastasmādatrapvayaḥ pātraṃ pratidhuk krūḍayati tat payasāgnihotraṃ juhotyamumeva tadādityaṃ juhotyeṣa hyevāgnihotramanaḍuho vai payastaṇḍulā asā ādityo'naṅvān yadyavāgvāgnihotraṃ juhotyamumeva tadādityaṃ juhotyeṣa hyevāgnihotraṃ cakravṛttamanyadacakravṛttamanyadyaccakravṛttaṃ tadasuryaṃ yadacakravṛttaṃ taddevamātraṃ tasmādacakravṛttāmagnihotratapanīṃ kurvīta || na kṣīreṇa pratiṣiñcennodakena yat kṣīreṇa pratiṣiñcedapratiṣiktam̐ syādyadudakena śamayeddohane sam̐srāvamucchiṣyeta nodakamiśreṇa pratiṣiñcet tanna kṣīreṇa pratiṣiktaṃ bhavati nodakena skannaṃ vāva tat pari cedākaroti yā sthālyagnihotratapanī tayā duhyādiyaṃ vai mānavā imāmiha nehātiskandatyanayaivopasīdati tadasyāskannaṃ bhavatyadbhiḥ pratiṣiñcatyāpo hi pratiṣecanāya tasthire tacchāntaṃ mithunamāpaśca payaśca yadi duhyamānāvabhidyādanyayā sthālyā duhyāt saiva tatra prāyaścittiryadyadhiśrīyamāṇaṃ yadyadhiśritaṃ skandedanyāmabhidudyādhiśrityonnīya juhuyāt saiva tatra prāyaścittiryadyunnīyamānaṃ yadyunītaṃ yadi pura upasannamahute skandet punaravanīyānyāmabhiduhyādhiśrityonnīya juhuyāt saiva tatra prāyaścittiryatra skandet tadapo ninayedāpo vai yajñasya niṣkṛtiradbhirevainaṃ niṣkaroti pratiṣiktamanyadapratiṣiktamanyat pratiṣiktaṃ paśukāmasyāpratiṣiktaṃ brahmavarcasakāmasya gharmo vā eṣa pravṛjyate yadagnihotraṃ yadapratiṣiktaṃ na juhoti brahmavarcasī bhavati yadi payo na vindedājyena juhuyāt taddhyapratiṣekyamapaśavyamīśvaramasyāśāntam̐ śucā paśūnnirdahaḥ pratiṣiktaṃ paśukāmasya tacchāntaṃ mithunamāpaśca mayaśca yadi payo na vindedyavāgvā juhuyāt taddhi pratiṣekyam̐ śāntaṃ mithunaṃ paśavyamāpaśca taṇḍulāśca || 3 ||

agnihotre vai jāyampatī vyabhicarete yat prācīnamudvāsayet patiḥ pūrvaḥ pramīyeta yat prācīnamudvāsayejjāyā pūrvā pramīyeta saṃpratyudīcīnamudvāsayet samevaṃ jīryataḥ pūrṇamagra unnayedatha saṃmitamatha saṃmitamatha saṃmitatamamuttamamanujyeṣṭhamevāsya putrā ṛdhnuvanti saṃmitamagra unnayedatha bhūyo'tha bhūyo'tha pūrṇamuttamaṃ bhūyo bhūya evānnādyamabhyutkrāmati kaniṣṭhastvasya putrāṇāmardhuko bhavati sarvān samāvadunnayedyaḥ kāmayeta sarve me putrāssamāvadṛdhnuyuriti sarveṣāṃ vai pitā putrāṇāmṛddhiṃ kāmayate sarva eva samāvadṛdhnuvanti || nānyo'nyamatikrāmati caturunnayati catuṣpada evāsmai paśūnna ... ... :::: ::: dvipada evāsmai paśūn yacchati paśūn me yaccheti paścādupasādayet paśavo vai gārhapatyaḥ paśavaḥ payaḥ paśūnāmevaiṣā yatirna paścādupasādayeddhavirvā etadyadagnihotraṃ yajamāno havirhavirbhūtameva yajamānamapanudata uttarādupasādayedapūtaṃ vā etadyadagnihotramagniḥ pavitraṃ yat samayāgnim̐ harati tenaivainat punātyanu vā eṣa etaddhyāyati yat paścādhiśritya puro juhoti yat samayāgnim̐ harati tenaivainaṃ prīṇātyanudhyāyinaṃ karotyasmādvai gārhapatyādasau pūrvo'gnirasṛjyatāyaṃ vāva prajāpatirasau pūrvo'gnistadayamita ādāya prāṅanūddrutyājuhot tasmādagnihotraṃ paścādhiśritya puro juhvati pura evānyāni havīm̐ṣi śṛtāni kurvanti puro juhvati || 4 ||

idhmo vā eṣo'gnihotrasya yat samidyat samidhamādadhātīdhmamevaitamagnihotrasya karoti rudra oṣadhīrviṣeṇālimpattāḥ paśavo nāliśanta tābhyo devāḥ prāyaścittimaicchan sa prajāpatirabravīdvāryaṃ vṛṇā ahaṃ ca etāssvadayiṣyāmīti sa etām̐ samidhamavṛṇīta tasmādekaiko hi prajāpatistā agninopāsṛjattā
agnirasvadayadetasyāṃ vā āhitāyāmagnihotriṇe vīrudhassvadanti svaditamasyānnaṃ bhavati ya evaṃ veda bahurdvīkarotyubhe āhutī samiddhe juhavānītyekaiva kāryā sakṛddhyeva sarvasmai yajñāya samidhyata āyurme yaccha varco me yaccha prajāṃ me yaccheti pura upasādayet sṛṣṭirvā etadyadagnihotraṃ yannopasādayet parācīḥ prajā iyuryadupasādayati tasmādidaṃ prajā yatāstasmādupatiṣṭhante'gnau jyotirjyotiragnā iti sāyamagnihotraṃ juhuyādgarbhiṇyā vācā garbhaṃ dadhāti mithunayā vācā garbhaṃ dadhāti sūryo jyotirjyotissūrya iti prātastaṃ garbhiṇyā vācā mithunayā prajanayati yanniruktaṃ cāniruktaṃ ca tanmithunaṃ yadyajuṣā ca manasā ca tanmithunaṃ tadajāmyāhutyorāgneyī sāyamāhutissaurī prātaragniḥ pravāpayitā sūryaḥ prajanayitāmnassūrye nimrukte sāyamagnihotraṃ juhuyādupodayam̐ sūryasya prātaragnireva pravāpayitvā sūryaṃ rātryai garbhaṃ dadhāti taṃ garbhiṇyā vācā mithunayā prātaḥ prajanayatyetaṃ vai prajāyamānaṃ prajā anuprajāyante pra prajayā pra paśubhirjāyate yasyaivamagnihotraṃ hūyata eṣā vā agnihotrasya sthāṇuryā pūrvāhutistāmatihāya juhuyāt tameva parivṛṇaktyabhikrāmam̐ sāyam̐ hotavyaṃ garbhameva dadhāti pratikrāmaṃ prātaḥ praiva janayati tadāhurabhikrāmameva hotavyamabhikrāntena hi yajñasyardhnoti yajjuhoti taddevānāṃ yaduddiśati tena rudram̐ śamayati yannimārṣṭi tat pitṝṇāṃ yat prāśnāti tanmanuṣyāṇāṃ tasmādagnihotraṃ vaiśvadevamucyate ||5||

praiyamedhā vai nāma brāhmaṇā āsam̐ste sarvamavidustat sahaivāviduste'gnihotra eva na samarādhayam̐steṣām̐ sakṛdeko'gnihotramajuhod dvirekastrirekasteṣāṃ yassakṛdajuhot tamitarā apṛcchatāṃ kasmai tvaṃ juhoṣītyekadhaivedamityabravīt prajāpatiḥ prajāpataya evāhaṃ juhomīti teṣāṃ yo dvirajuhot tamitarā apṛcchatāṃ kasmai tvaṃ juhoṣītyagnaye caiva sāyaṃ prajāpataye cetyabravīt sūryāya ca prātaḥ prajāpataye ceti teṣāṃ yastrirajuhot tamitarā apṛcchatāṃ kasmai tvaṃ juhoṣītyagnaye sūryāya prajāpataya ityabravīt tebhya eva sāyaṃ juhomi tebhyaḥ prātariti teṣāṃ yo dvirajuhot sa ārdhnot sa bhūyiṣṭho'bhavat | prajayātītarau śriyākrāmat tasya prajāmitarayoḥ praje sajātatvamupetāṃ tasmād dve hotavye yajuṣā ca manasā ca yāmeva sa ṛddhimārdhnot tāmṛdhnoti yadi sāyamahute'gnihotre pūrvo'gniranugacchedagnihotramadhiśrityonnīyāgninā pūrveṇoddrutyāgnihotreṇānūdravedāhutyaivainaṃ cyāvayati yo brāhmaṇo bahuvitsyāt sa uddharet sarveṇaivainaṃ brahmaṇoddharati yat purā dhanamadāyī syāt taddadyādacyutenaivainaṃ cyāvayati yadi prātarahute'gnihotre'paro'gniranugacchedanugamayitvā pūrvaṃ mathitvāparamuddhṛtya juhuyāt saiva tatra prāyaścittiryadi tvareta || pūrvamagnimanvavasāya tataḥ prāñcamuddhṛtya juhuyāt saiva tatra prāyaścittirjāmi nu tadyo'sya pūrvo'gnistamaparaṃ karotyanyatraivāvasāyāgniṃ mathitvoddhṛtya juhuyāt saiva tatra prāyaścittastataśśvo'gnaye tapasvate janadvate pāvakavata iṣṭiṃ nivepedyadā vā agnissaṃtapyate'tha jāyata oṣadhayaḥ pāvakā oṣadhīrevāsmai pāvakā bhāgadheyamapidadhāti na gārhamanubudhyate yadā hyevāsmai nāpidadhatyathaiṣo'nugacchati yau panthānā abhitasthau darśapūrṇamāsau yā madhye sṛtistadagnihotramevaṃ vā agnihotriṇe darśapūrṇamāsine svargo loko'nubhāti || manasā vā imāṃ prajāpatirvediṃ paryagṛhṇādagnihotriṇe tatra yat kiṃca dadāti tadbarhiṣyaṃ yathāsanneṣu nārāśam̐seṣu dadātyevameṣa dadāti yatkiṃca dadāti madhya eva yajñasya dyāvāpṛthivī etasya sadohavirdhāne ahorātrāṇīdhmo diśaḥ paridhayaḥ pruṣvāḥ prokṣaṇīroṣadhayo na hi yajamāno yūpo na saṃbhavan vadet paśureva bhūtvā saṃbhavatyaharaharvai paśavassaṃbhavanto'tha medhyā na rājanyasyāgnihotramastyavratyo hi sa hanti vrataṃ na vicchindyāt paurṇamāsīṃ ca rātrīmamāvasyāṃ ca juhuyāt te hi vrataṃ gopāyati yānyahāni na juhuyāt tānyasya brāhmaṇāyāgne gṛha upahareyuragnirvai brāhmaṇo'gnā eva tajjuhoti tadasya svaditameṣṭaṃ bhavati || 6 ||

vācā vai saha manuṣyā ajāyanta te vāco devāścāsurāśca te yanmanuṣyā avadam̐stadevābhavante devāścāsurāśca prajāpatimabruvannime vāvedamabhūvanniti sa vācassatyaṃ niramimīta bhūrbhuvassvariti yatturīyamanṛtaṃ tanmanuṣyeṣu nyadadhādetadvai vāco'nṛtaṃ yanmanuṣyā vadanti bhūritīmāmasṛjatāgniṃ rathaṃtaraṃ trivṛtaṃ gāyatrīṃ bhuvarityantarikṣaṃ vātaṃ vāmadevyaṃ triṣṭubhaṃ pañcadaśa svariti divam̐ sūryaṃ bṛhadekaviśaṃ jagatīṃ bhūrbhuvassvaragnau jyotirjyotiragnā ityāgnihotraṃ juhuyādetadvai vācassatyametanmithunametadbrahma tenaiva juhotīmā evaitadiṣṭakā upadhatta imā evaitadiṣṭakā upadhāyottamaṃ nākaṃ rohatyuttamassamānānāṃ bhavati yasyaivamagnihotram̐ hūyate || vācaṃ yacchedagnihotra unnīyamāne tapasā vai prajāpatiḥ prajā asṛjata yanna vyāharat tadevātapyata sṛṣṭirvā etadyadagnihotram̐ sṛṣṭiretadvrataṃ tā udbhavassthetyudabhāvayadyarhi bindava iva syustarhyavekṣetodbhavassthodahaṃ prajayā pra paśubhirbhūyāsamityudbhūtirvā etat prabhūtiryadagnihotramutprajayā pra paśubhirbhavati yasyaivamagnihotram̐ hūyate'gnināvekṣate prajananaṃ vā etadyadagnihotramagniḥ prajanayitā praiva janayati|| reto vā etadyadagnihotraṃ na suśṛtaṃ kuryād retaḥ krūḍayenno aśṛtamantareṇaiva syādrudra mṛḍānārbhava mṛḍa dhūrte namaste'stviti hutvodaṅṅuddiśedetāni vai rudrasya krūrāṇi nāmāni taireṣa prajā hinastyagnihotre bhāgadheyamicchamānastānyevāsya pratinudati tāni śamayati yarhyayaṃ devāḥ prajā abhimanyeta sajūrjātavedo divā pṛthivyā haviṣo vīhi svāheti dvādaśa rātrīragnihotraṃ juhuyādyā vā agnerjātavedāstanūstayaiṣa prajā hinastyagnihotre bhāgadheyamicchamānastāmevāsya prīṇāti tasyaitajjuhoti| tadajāmyāhutyorāgneyyeṣāgneyītarā saṃvatsaraṃ vā etamindhate'gniṃ vaiśvānaraṃ na vā etasya saṃvatsare'pyasti na vaiśvānare yo'nāhitāgniryāni dārūṇi te māsā ye sthūlā aṅgārāste ṛtavo ye'ṇīyām̐saste'rdhamāsā murmurā ahorātrāṇi yat suvarṇa jyotistadbārhaspatyaṃ yanna suvarṇaṃ na lohitaṃ tanmaitraṃ yallohitaṃ tadvāruṇaṃ yatsadhūmaṃ tadvaiśvadevaṃ yatrāṅgāreṣvagnirlelāyeva tadasyāsyamāvirnāma tadbrahma tasmin hotavyaṃ mukhena vā aśnātyaṅgānyavatyāviḥ prajāsu bhavati śvaśśvo vasīyān bhavati yasyaivamagnihotraṃ hūyate || 7 ||

odanapacano gārhapatya āhavanīyo madhyādhidevanamāmantraṇameṣā vai virāṭ pañcapadā tāmevāpnoti tāmavarunddhe yasya hyeṣāvaruddhā sa manuṣyāṇām̐ śreṣṭho bhavati yadi sāyamagnihotrasya kālo'tipadyeta doṣā vastossvāheti juhuyāt saiva tatrāhutistenāsya tadanatipannaṃ bhavati yadi prātaragnihotrasya kālo'tipadyeta divā vastossvāheti juhuyāt saiva tatrāhutistenāsya tadanatipannaṃ bhavati na svāhākāro vā agnihotrasyāhutiṃ yuvate yarhi vāva pravadet tarhi juhuyāt sa hyagnihotrasya vaṣaṭkārassajūragnirdivā pṛthivyeti dyāvāpṛthivī agniṃ tāneva prīṇāti tānavarunddhe sajūrdevena sāvitreti na hi saviturṛta āhutirasti saja़ūḥ pūṣṇāntarikṣeṇeti paśavo vai pūṣā paśavo'ntarikṣaṃ paśūnevāvarundre saja़ū rātryendravatyā svāheti rātrīmevaitadabhijuhoti na hīndrādṛt āhutirasti devā vai purāgnihotramahauṣustasmāt purā bṛhanmahānajani sajūrahnendravatā svāheti bārhataṃ vā aharbārhatassūryo'haścaivāsyaitat sūryaścābhijitā abhihutau bhavata ānuṣṭubhī vai rātrī traiṣṭubhamaho rātrīṃ caivaitadahaścābhijuhotyagne'dābhya juṣasva svāhetyuttarāmāhutiṃ juhoti yathāgnaye samavadyatyevameva tadapipreragne svāṃ tanvamayān dyāvāpṛthivī ūrjamasmāsu dhehīti tṛṇamaktvānupraharati sam̐sthitirevaiṣā svagākṛtiragnihotrasya vā eṣo'śāntamanu prajā hinasti yattṛṇamaktvānu praharati tenaivainacchamayatyagne gṛhapate juṣasva svāheti sruveṇa gārhapatye juhoti yathā patnīssaṃyājayatyevameva tadanu vā eṣa etaddhyāyati yat paścādhiśritya purā juhoti yat sruveṇa gārhapatye juhoti tenaivainaṃ prīṇātyananudhyāyinaṃ karotyasyāgnihotrī prajāyāṃ jāyate śvaśśvo vasīyān bhavati yasyaivamagnihotram̐ hūyate || 8 ||

upaprayanto adhvaraṃ mantraṃ vocemāgnaye | āre asme ca śṛṇvate ||
asya pratnāmanu dyutam̐ śukraṃ duduhre ahrayaḥ | payassahasrasāmṛṣim ||
agnirmūrdhā divaḥ kakutpatiḥ pṛthivyā ayam | apāṃ retām̐si jinvati ||
ubhā vāmindrāgnī āhuvadhyā ubhā rādhasassaha mādayadhyai ||
ubhā dātārā iṣāṃ rayīṇāmubhā vājasya sātaye huve vām ||
ayamiha prathamo dhāyi dhātṛbhirhotā yajiṣṭho adhvareṣvīḍyaḥ ||
yamapnavāno bhṛgavo virurucurvaneṣu citraṃ vibhvaṃ viśe viśe ||
ayaṃ te yonirṛtviyo yato jāto arocathāḥ ||
taṃ jānannagna āroha tato no vardhayā rayim ||
dadhikrāvṇo akāriṣaṃ jiṣṇoraśvasya vājinaḥ ||
surabhi no mukhā karat pra ṇa āyūṃṣi tāriṣat ||
tvamagne sūryavarcāssaṃ māmayuṣā varcasā sṛja saṃ tvamagne sūryasya jyotiṣāgathāssamṛṣīṇām̐ stutena saṃ priyeṇa dhāmnā samahamāyuṣā saṃ varcasā saṃ prajayā saṃ rāyaspoṣeṇa gmīya ||
indhānāstvā śatam̐ hiṃmā dyumantassamidhīmahi |
vayasvanto vayasvinaṃ yaśasvanto yaśasvinam |
suvīrāso adābhyamagne sapatnadambhanam ||
āyurdhā agne'syāyurme dhehi vayodhā agne'si vayo me dhehi tanūpā agne'si tanvaṃ me pāhi || yanme agna ūnaṃ tanvastanma āpṛṇāgnessamidasyabhiśastyā mā pāhi somasya samidasi paraspā ma edhi yamasya samidasi mṛtyormā pāhyagne yatte tapastena taṃ pratitapa yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ ca vayaṃ dviṣmo'gne yatte śocistena taṃ pratiśoca yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ ca vayaṃ dviṣmo'gne yatte tejastena taṃ pratitityagdhi yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ ca vayaṃ dviṣmo'gne yatte'rcistena taṃ pratyarca yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ ca vayaṃ dviṣmo'gne yatte harastena taṃ pratihara yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ ca vayaṃ dviṣmo'gne rucāṃ pate namaste ruce mayi rucaṃ dhehi citrāvaso svasti te pāramaśīyārvāgvaso svasti te pāramaśīya || 9 ||

vetthā hi vedho adhvanaḥ pathaśca devāñjasā | agne yajñeṣu sukrato ||
agne naya supathā rāye asmān viśvāni deva vayunāni vidvān |
yuyodhyasmajjuhurāṇameno bhūyiṣṭhāṃ te namauktiṃ vidhema ||
ā tū na indra vṛtrahannasmākamardhamāgahi | mahānmahībhirūtibhiḥ ||
tvaṃ mahām̐ indra tubhyam̐ ha kṣā anu kṣatraṃ mam̐hanā manyata dyauḥ |
tvaṃ vṛtram̐ śavasā jaghanvān sṛjassidhūm̐rahinā jagrasānān ||
ṛtāvānaṃ vaiśvānaram || vaiśvānarasya sumatau syāma ||
agne dā dāśuṣe rayiṃ vīravantaṃ parīṇasam | śiśīhi nassūnumataḥ ||
tvaddhi putra sahaso vi pūrvīrdevasya yantūtayo vi vājāḥ ||
tvaṃ dehi sahasriṇaṃ rayiṃ no'droghena vacasā satyamagne |
dīrghaste astvaṅkuśo yenā vasu prayacchasi | yajamānāya sunvate ||
ā tū bhara mākiretat pariṣṭhādvidmā hi tvā vasupatiṃ vasūnām ||
indra yatte māhinaṃ datramastyasmabhyaṃ taddharyaśva prayandhi ||
pra tatte adya śipiviṣṭa nāmāryaśśam̐sāmi vayunāni vidvān ||
taṃ tvā gṛṇāmi tavasamatavyān kṣayantamasya rajasaḥ parāke ||
vaṣaṭ te viṣṇa āsa ā kṛṇomi tanme juṣasva śipiviṣṭa havyam |
vardhantu tvā suṣṭutayo giro me yūyaṃ pāta svastibhissadā naḥ ||
tyāmagne vājasātamaṃ viprā vardhanti suṣṭatam | sa no rāsva suvīryam ||
ayaṃ no agnirvarivaḥ kṛṇotvayaṃ mṛdhaḥ pura etu prabhindan |
ayaṃ vājaṃ jayatu vājasātā ayam̐ śatrūñjayatu jarhṛṣāṇaḥ ||
tvamagne vratapā asi || yadvo vayam || 10 ||

kṛṇuṣva pājaḥ ||
sa te jānāti sumatiṃ yaviṣṭha ya īvate brahmaṇe gātumairat ||
viśvānyasmai sudināni rāyo dyumnānyaryo vi duro abhidyaut ||
sedagne astu subhagassudānuryastvā nityena haviṣā ya ukthaiḥ |
piprīpati sva āyuṣi duroṇe viśvedasmai sudinā sāsadiṣṭiḥ ||
arcāmi te sumatiṃ ghoṣyarvāk saṃ te vāvātā jaratāmiyaṃ gīḥ |
svaśvāstvā surathā mārjayemāsme kṣatrāṇi dhārayeranu dyūn |
iha tvā bhūryācaredupa tman doṣāvastardīdivām̐samanu dyūn |
krīḍantastvā sumanasassapemābhi dyumnā tasthivām̐so janānām ||
yastvā svaśvassahiraṇyo agna upayāti vasumatā rathena ||
tasya trātā bhavasi tasya sakhā yasta ātithyamānuṣag jujoṣat ||
maho rujāmi bandhutā vacobhistanmā piturgotamādanviyāya |
tvaṃ no asya vacasaścikiddhi hotaryaviṣṭha sukrato damūnāḥ ||
asvapnajastaraṇayassuśevā atandrāso'vṛkā aśramiṣṭhāḥ ||
te pāyavassadhryañco niṣadyāgne tava naḥ pāntvamūra ||
ye pāyavo māmateyaṃ te agne paśyanto andhaṃ duritādarakṣan |
rarakṣa tān sukṛto viśvavedā dipsanta idripavo nāha debhuḥ ||
tvayā vayam̐ sadhanya2stvotāstava praṇītyaśyāma vājān |
ubhā śam̐sā sūdaya satyatāte'nuṣṭhuyā kṛṇuhyahrayāṇa ||
ayā te agne samidhā vidhema prati stomam̐ śasyamānaṃ gṛbhāya |
dahāśaṃso rakṣasaḥ pāhyasmān druho nidā mitramaho avadyāt || 11 || 


iti śrīyajuṣi kāṭhake carakaśākhāyāmiṭhimikāyāmagnihotrabrāhmaṇaṃ nāma ṣaṣṭhaṃ sthānakaṃ saṃpūrṇam ||6||
<7>
atha saptamaṃ sthānakam |

ālobhī |
ambhasthāmbho vo bhakṣīya mahasstha maho vo bhakṣīyorjassthorjaṃ vo bhakṣīya rāyaspoṣasstha rāyaspoṣaṃ vo bhakṣīya revatī ramadhvamasminyonā asmin goṣṭhe'yaṃ vo bandhurito māpagāta mā mā hāsiṣṭa bahvīrme bhavata sam̐hitāsi viśvarūpā morjā viśā gaupatyenā prajayā rāyaspoṣeṇa mayi vo rāyaśśrayantām̐ sahasrapoṣaṃ vaḥ puṣeyam ||
saṃpaśyāmi prajā ahamiḍāprajaso mānavīḥ | bahvībhavantu no gṛhe ||
upa tvāgne dive dive doṣāvastardhiyā vayam | namo bharanta emasi ||
rājantamadhvarāṇāṃ gopāmṛtasya dīdivim | vardhamānam̐ sve dame ||
sa naḥ piteva sūnave'gne sūpāyano bhava | sacasvā nassvastaye ||
agne tvaṃ no antama uta trātā śivo bhavā varūthyaḥ |
taṃ tvā śociṣṭha dīdivassumnāya nūnamīmahe sakhibhyaḥ ||
vasuragnirvasuśravā acchā nakṣi dyumattamaṃ rayiṃ dāḥ ||
sa no bodhi śrudhī havamuruṣyā ṇo aghāyatassamasmāt ||
ūrjā vaḥ paśyāmyūrjā mā paśyata rāyaspoṣeṇa vaḥ paśyāmi rāyaspoṣeṇa mā paśyateḍāsstha madhukṛtassyonā māviśateraṃmado mayi vo rāyaśśrayantām̐ sahasrapoṣaṃ vaḥ puṣeyam || 1 ||

mahi trīṇāmavo'stu dyukṣaṃ mitrasyāryamṇaḥ | durādharṣaṃ varuṇasya ||
nahi teṣāmamā cana nādhvasu vāraṇeṣu | īśe ripuraghaśam̐saḥ |
yasmai putrāso aditeḥ pra jīvase martyāya | jyotiryacchantyajasram ||
somānam̐ svaraṇaṃ kṛṇuhi brahmaṇaspate | kakṣīvantaṃ ya auśijaḥ ||
yo revanyo amīvahā vasuvit puṣṭivardhanaḥ | sa nassiṣaktu yasturaḥ ||
mā naśśam̐so araruṣo dhūrtiḥ praṇaṅ martyasya | rakṣā ṇo brahmaṇaspate ||
kadā cana starīrasi nendra saścasi dāśuṣe |
upopennu maghavan bhūya innu te dānaṃ devasya pṛcyate |||
pari te dūḍabho ratho'smām̐ aśnotu viśvataḥ | yena rakṣasi dāśuṣaḥ ||
acchinno daivyastanturmā manuṣyaśchedi nimrado'sīdamahaṃ taṃ nimṛṇāmi yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ ca vayaṃ dviṣmo vibhūrasyabhyahaṃ taṃ bhūyāsaṃ yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ ca vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ prabhūrasi prāhaṃ tamatibhūyāsaṃ yo'smān dveṣṭi yaṃ ca vayaṃ dviṣmaḥ pūṣā mā prapathe pātu pūṣā mā paśupāḥ pātu pūṣā mādhipatiḥ pātu prācī digagnirdevatāgniṃ sa ṛcchatu yo maitasyā diśo'bhidāsati dakṣiṇā digindro devatedram̐ sa ṛcchatu yo maitasyā diśo'bhidāsati pratīcī diksomo devatā somam̐ sa ṛcchatu yo maitasyā diśo'bhidāsatyudīcī diṅ mitrāvaruṇau devatā mitrāvaruṇau sa ṛcchatu yo maitasyā diśo'bhidāsatyūrdhvā digbṛhaspatirdevatā bṛhaspatiṃ sa ṛcchatu yo maitasyā diśo'bhidāsatīyaṃ digaditirdevatāditiṃ sa ṛcchatu yā maitasyā diśo'bhidāsati jyotiṣe tantava āśiṣamāśāste'sā anu mā tanu || 2 ||

dhanaṃ me śam̐sya pāhi tanme gopāya ||
mama nāma prathamaṃ jātavedā mātā pitā ca dadhaturnvagre |
tattvaṃ gopāyā punardadai te vayaṃ bibharāma tava nāma ||
prajāṃ me narya pāhi tāṃ me gopāya ||
imān me mitrāvaruṇau gṛhān gopāyataṃ yuvam |
avinaṣṭānavihrutān pūṣainānabhirakṣatvāsmākaṃ punarāgamāt ||
annaṃ me purīṣya pāhi tanme gopāya dhanaṃ me śam̐syājugupastanme punardehi || agne sahasrākṣa śatamūrdhañchatatejaśśataṃ te prāṇāssahasraṃ vyānāḥ || tvam̐ sāhasrasya rāya īśiṣe tasya no rāsva tasya te bhaktivāno bhūyāsma ||
mama ca nāma tava jātavedo vāsasī iva vivasānau carāvaḥ ||
te bibhṛvo dakṣase jīvase ca yathāyathaṃ nau tanvau jātavedaḥ ||
prajāṃ me naryājugupastāṃ me punardehyagne gṛhapate sugṛhapatirahaṃ tvayā gṛhapatinā bhūyāsam̐ sugṛhapatirmayā tvaṃ gṛhapatinā bhūyā asthūri ṇau gārhapatyaṃ dīdāyacchatam̐ himādvāyū annaṃ me purīṣyājugupastanme punardehi || 3 ||

agnihotre stomo yoktavyo yasya vā agnihotre stomo yujyate vasīyān bhavatyupaprayanto adhvaramiti prajā vā imā upa tāssatsatstomastā evopayunaktyasya pratnāmanu dyutamiti devā vai pratnaṃ te satsatstomastāneva prāyukta svargāyopopennu maghavan bhūya innu ta itīyaṃ vā upa svargamevetvāsyāṃ pratitiṣṭhati yā vai prajā ābhaviṣyantīstā upa tā evopayunakti pari te dūḍabho ratha iti stomamevaitayā yuktaṃ parigṛhṇātyagnirmūrdhā divaḥ kakudityeṣā mithunā retasvatī paśavyā yāsau dhurāṃ gāyatrī prathamā saiṣā gāyatryopāsthitobhā vāmindrāgnī āhuvadhyā iti sūryo vā indrasso'gniṃ naktaṃ prāviśatyubhā evainau saheṭṭe yāsau dhurāṃ triṣṭupprathamā saiṣā triṣṭubhopāsthitāyāmiha prathamo dhāyi dhātṛbhirityagnirhyevāsmiṃlloka iha prathamo'dhīyata yāsau dhurāṃ jagatī prathamā saiṣā jagatyopāsthitāyaṃ te yonirṛtviya itīyam̐ hyagneryoniragnissūryasya yāsau dhurāmanuṣṭupprathamā saiṣānuṣṭubhopāsthita || dadhikrāvṇo akāriṣamityeṣā vā agneḥ paśavyā tanūryā dadhikrāvatī tāmevāsya gṛhṇātyupasthāyukā enaṃ paśavo bhavanti paśurvā agniryathā vai gaurjīryati yathāśvo yathā puruṣa evameṣa āhito jīryatyagnyādheyasyargbhirupastheyastanvo vā asyaitāstābhirevainaṃ punarṇavaṃ karoti yacchandobhirupatiṣṭhate tairevainaṃ punarṇavaṃ karoti yathāgra āhita evamasya bhavatyupatiṣṭhate yoga evasyaiṣa dama evāsyaiṣa yācña evāsyaiṣa yathā śreyasa āhṛtya namasyatyevameva tat || 4 ||
